# Voxel2Pixel
Voxel2Pixel is an [MIT-licensed](LICENSE) [C# 13.0](https://dotnet.microsoft.com/en-us/languages/csharp) [.NET Standard 2.0](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/standard/net-standard) library created by Ben McLean for rendering [voxel models](https://www.megavoxels.com/learn/what-is-a-voxel/) as [pixel art assets](https://2dwillneverdie.com/intro/) from a variety of fixed [camera perspectives](https://opengameart.org/content/chapter-3-perspectives) and [palette limitations](https://lospec.com/palette-list) intended for use in retro-styled video games.

Also in this repo is the [BenVoxel project](BenVoxel/README.md) to develop an open standard for interoperable voxel model file formats.
## Usage
### Importing a model
```csharp
VoxFileModel model = new(@"..\..\..\Sora.vox");
IVoxelColor voxelColor = new NaiveDimmer(model.Palette);
```
The `VoxFileModel` class is used for importing [MagicaVoxel](https://ephtracy.github.io/) files. The `Sora.vox` file contains a test model based on the character Sora from Kingdom Hearts.

Colors are specified in [RGBA8888 format](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RGBA_color_model#RGBA8888). The `IVoxelColor` interface is used to determine which colors to draw sprites with. The `NaiveDimmer` class implements `IVoxelColor` by interpolating lighter and darker versions from the provided palette, which in this snippet comes from the imported MagicaVoxel file.
### Drawing a sprite
```csharp
Sprite sprite = new SpriteMaker
{
	Model = model,
	VoxelColor = new NaiveDimmer(model.Palette),
	Perspective = Perspective.Iso,
	Outline = true,
	ScaleX = 2,
}.Make();
byte[] texture = sprite.Texture;
ushort width = sprite.Width,
	height = sprite.Height;
```
The `SpriteMaker` class uses [builder pattern](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Builder_pattern) to construct `Sprite` objects.

The `Sprite` class provides a `byte[] Texture` array containing [RGBA8888 format](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RGBA_color_model#RGBA8888) pixels and a `ushort Width` property for the texture's size. These would be referenced to export images.
### Creating a texture atlas
```csharp
Dictionary<string, Sprite> sprites = [];
byte direction = 0;
foreach (SpriteMaker spriteMaker in new SpriteMaker()
	{
		Model = model,
		VoxelColor = new NaiveDimmer(model.Palette),
		Outline = true,
	}.Iso8())
	sprites.Add("Sprite" + direction++, spriteMaker.Make());
Sprite output = new(sprites, out TextureAtlas textureAtlas);
```
The `SpriteMaker.Iso8` method generates makers for a series of 8 sprites using alternating isometric and 3/4ths camera perspectives from the 8 winds of the compass.

The sprites are then placed into a `Dictionary<string, Sprite>` data structure to associate each sprite with a unique name.

Finally, all the sprites are combined into one texture atlas, where `output` is a `Sprite` instance containing the texture and `textureAtlas` contains the metadata.

The `Voxel2Pixel.Test` project also depends on [SixLabors.ImageSharp](https://github.com/SixLabors/ImageSharp), [Magick.NET](https://github.com/dlemstra/Magick.NET) and [SkiaSharp](https://github.com/mono/SkiaSharp) for image file output, but the actual Voxel2Pixel library does not include these.

Note that using the other non-test projects without the web front-end does not impose the conditions of the Six Labors Split License.

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/BoundsCompute.cs
```
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using BenVoxel.Models;
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

/// <summary>
/// Manages GPU compute shader for bounds calculation.
/// Processes segment textures to find min/max voxel coordinates.
///
/// Synchronous execution with throttling - GPU parallelism makes this fast.
/// Godot 4 doesn't support true async GPU readback, so we embrace the sync
/// but benefit from GPU parallelism for the actual computation.
/// </summary>
public sealed class BoundsCompute : IDisposable
{
	// Bounds result: 6 uints (minX, maxX, minY, maxY, minZ, maxZ) per segment
	private const int BoundsPerSegment = 6;
	private const int MaxSegments = 256;

	private readonly RenderingDevice _rd;
	private Rid _shader;
	private Rid _pipeline;
	private Rid _boundsBuffer;

	// Throttling to avoid computing every frame
	private double _lastTrimTime;
	private const double ThrottleIntervalSeconds = 0.1; // Max once per 100ms

	/// <summary>
	/// Whether the compute shader is available and initialized.
	/// </summary>
	public bool IsAvailable => _shader.IsValid && _pipeline.IsValid && _boundsBuffer.IsValid;

	public BoundsCompute()
	{
		// Use the main RenderingDevice to access textures from the rendering pipeline
		// via RenderingServer.TextureGetRdTexture()
		_rd = RenderingServer.GetRenderingDevice();
		if (_rd == null)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: RenderingDevice not available");
			return;
		}

		InitializeShader();
		InitializeBuffers();
	}

	private void InitializeShader()
	{
		// Load and compile the compute shader
		RDShaderFile shaderFile = GD.Load<RDShaderFile>("res://bounds_reduction.glsl");
		if (shaderFile == null)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: Failed to load bounds_reduction.glsl");
			return;
		}

		RDShaderSpirV spirv = shaderFile.GetSpirV();
		if (spirv == null)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: Failed to get SPIR-V from shader");
			return;
		}

		_shader = _rd.ShaderCreateFromSpirV(spirv);
		if (!_shader.IsValid)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: Failed to create shader");
			return;
		}

		_pipeline = _rd.ComputePipelineCreate(_shader);
		if (!_pipeline.IsValid)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: Failed to create compute pipeline");
			return;
		}

		GD.Print("BoundsCompute: Shader initialized successfully");
	}

	private void InitializeBuffers()
	{
		if (!_shader.IsValid) return;

		// Create output buffer for bounds results
		// 6 uints per segment × max segments × 4 bytes per uint
		int bufferSize = BoundsPerSegment * MaxSegments * sizeof(uint);
		byte[] initialData = new byte[bufferSize];

		_boundsBuffer = _rd.StorageBufferCreate((uint)bufferSize, initialData);
		if (!_boundsBuffer.IsValid)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: Failed to create bounds buffer");
			return;
		}

		GD.Print($"BoundsCompute: Created bounds buffer ({bufferSize} bytes)");
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Computes bounds using GPU, with throttling.
	/// Returns null if throttled, empty, or not available.
	/// </summary>
	/// <param name="segmentTextures">Segment textures already on GPU</param>
	/// <param name="directory">Segment directory</param>
	/// <param name="force">If true, bypasses throttling</param>
	/// <returns>Computed bounds, or null</returns>
	public SegmentedBrickModel.Bounds? ComputeBounds(
		IReadOnlyDictionary<uint, ImageTexture3D> segmentTextures,
		IReadOnlyList<VoxelBridge.SegmentEntry> directory,
		bool force = false)
	{
		if (!IsAvailable)
			return null;

		// Throttle unless forced
		if (!force)
		{
			double now = Time.GetTicksMsec() / 1000.0;
			if (now - _lastTrimTime < ThrottleIntervalSeconds)
				return null;
			_lastTrimTime = now;
		}

		if (directory.Count == 0)
			return null;

		// Clear the bounds buffer to initial values
		ClearBoundsBuffer();

		// Dispatch compute for each segment
		DispatchCompute(segmentTextures, directory);

		// Note: On main RenderingDevice, we can't call Submit/Sync.
		// BufferGetData should implicitly wait for compute to complete.
		return ReadResults(directory.Count);
	}

	private void ClearBoundsBuffer()
	{
		// Initialize buffer with max values for min and 0 for max
		// This allows atomic min/max to work correctly
		uint[] initialValues = new uint[BoundsPerSegment * MaxSegments];
		for (int i = 0; i < MaxSegments; i++)
		{
			int baseIdx = i * BoundsPerSegment;
			initialValues[baseIdx + 0] = uint.MaxValue; // minX
			initialValues[baseIdx + 1] = 0;             // maxX
			initialValues[baseIdx + 2] = uint.MaxValue; // minY
			initialValues[baseIdx + 3] = 0;             // maxY
			initialValues[baseIdx + 4] = uint.MaxValue; // minZ
			initialValues[baseIdx + 5] = 0;             // maxZ
		}

		byte[] bytes = MemoryMarshal.AsBytes(initialValues.AsSpan()).ToArray();
		_rd.BufferUpdate(_boundsBuffer, 0, (uint)bytes.Length, bytes);
	}

	private void DispatchCompute(
		IReadOnlyDictionary<uint, ImageTexture3D> segmentTextures,
		IReadOnlyList<VoxelBridge.SegmentEntry> directory)
	{
		long computeList = _rd.ComputeListBegin();

		for (int i = 0; i < directory.Count; i++)
		{
			VoxelBridge.SegmentEntry entry = directory[i];
			uint segmentId = ((uint)entry.X << 18) | ((uint)entry.Y << 9) | (uint)entry.Z;

			if (!segmentTextures.TryGetValue(segmentId, out ImageTexture3D texture))
				continue;

			// Get the texture's RID and convert to RenderingDevice-compatible RID
			Rid highLevelRid = texture.GetRid();
			if (!highLevelRid.IsValid)
				continue;

			// Bridge from high-level texture to RenderingDevice texture
			Rid textureRid = RenderingServer.TextureGetRdTexture(highLevelRid);
			if (!textureRid.IsValid)
				continue;

			// Create sampler for the texture
			RDSamplerState samplerState = new()
			{
				MinFilter = RenderingDevice.SamplerFilter.Nearest,
				MagFilter = RenderingDevice.SamplerFilter.Nearest,
				MipFilter = RenderingDevice.SamplerFilter.Nearest,
				RepeatU = RenderingDevice.SamplerRepeatMode.ClampToEdge,
				RepeatV = RenderingDevice.SamplerRepeatMode.ClampToEdge,
				RepeatW = RenderingDevice.SamplerRepeatMode.ClampToEdge
			};
			Rid sampler = _rd.SamplerCreate(samplerState);

			// Create uniform set for this segment
			Godot.Collections.Array<RDUniform> uniforms = [];

			// Binding 0: Segment texture (sampler3D)
			RDUniform textureUniform = new()
			{
				UniformType = RenderingDevice.UniformType.SamplerWithTexture,
				Binding = 0
			};
			textureUniform.AddId(sampler);
			textureUniform.AddId(textureRid);
			uniforms.Add(textureUniform);

			// Binding 1: Bounds buffer
			RDUniform bufferUniform = new()
			{
				UniformType = RenderingDevice.UniformType.StorageBuffer,
				Binding = 1
			};
			bufferUniform.AddId(_boundsBuffer);
			uniforms.Add(bufferUniform);

			Rid uniformSet = _rd.UniformSetCreate(uniforms, _shader, 0);

			// Prepare push constants
			// segment_index, segment_origin_x, segment_origin_y, segment_origin_z
			uint[] pushConstants =
			[
				(uint)i,
				(uint)(entry.X * 128),
				(uint)(entry.Y * 128),
				(uint)(entry.Z * 128)
			];
			byte[] pushConstantBytes = MemoryMarshal.AsBytes(pushConstants.AsSpan()).ToArray();

			// Bind and dispatch
			_rd.ComputeListBindComputePipeline(computeList, _pipeline);
			_rd.ComputeListBindUniformSet(computeList, uniformSet, 0);
			_rd.ComputeListSetPushConstant(computeList, pushConstantBytes, (uint)pushConstantBytes.Length);

			// Each workgroup covers a 64³ block of voxels, so a 128³ segment is 2×2×2 workgroups
			_rd.ComputeListDispatch(computeList, 2, 2, 2);

			// Add barrier between segments to ensure writes complete before next segment
			_rd.ComputeListAddBarrier(computeList);
		}

		// Final barrier to ensure all writes are complete before we read
		_rd.ComputeListAddBarrier(computeList);
		_rd.ComputeListEnd();
	}

	private SegmentedBrickModel.Bounds? ReadResults(int segmentCount)
	{
		byte[] data = _rd.BufferGetData(_boundsBuffer);
		if (data == null || data.Length == 0)
			return null;

		uint[] values = MemoryMarshal.Cast<byte, uint>(data).ToArray();

		// Combine results from all segments
		uint globalMinX = uint.MaxValue, globalMaxX = 0;
		uint globalMinY = uint.MaxValue, globalMaxY = 0;
		uint globalMinZ = uint.MaxValue, globalMaxZ = 0;
		bool hasAnyVoxels = false;

		for (int i = 0; i < segmentCount; i++)
		{
			int baseIdx = i * BoundsPerSegment;
			uint minX = values[baseIdx + 0];
			uint maxX = values[baseIdx + 1];
			uint minY = values[baseIdx + 2];
			uint maxY = values[baseIdx + 3];
			uint minZ = values[baseIdx + 4];
			uint maxZ = values[baseIdx + 5];

			// Check if this segment had any voxels (maxX >= minX means valid)
			if (maxX >= minX && maxY >= minY && maxZ >= minZ)
			{
				hasAnyVoxels = true;
				globalMinX = Math.Min(globalMinX, minX);
				globalMaxX = Math.Max(globalMaxX, maxX);
				globalMinY = Math.Min(globalMinY, minY);
				globalMaxY = Math.Max(globalMaxY, maxY);
				globalMinZ = Math.Min(globalMinZ, minZ);
				globalMaxZ = Math.Max(globalMaxZ, maxZ);
			}
		}

		if (!hasAnyVoxels)
			return null;

		return new SegmentedBrickModel.Bounds(
			(ushort)globalMinX, (ushort)globalMaxX,
			(ushort)globalMinY, (ushort)globalMaxY,
			(ushort)globalMinZ, (ushort)globalMaxZ);
	}

	public void Dispose()
	{
		if (_boundsBuffer.IsValid)
			_rd.FreeRid(_boundsBuffer);
		if (_pipeline.IsValid)
			_rd.FreeRid(_pipeline);
		if (_shader.IsValid)
			_rd.FreeRid(_shader);
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/FreeLookCamera.cs
```
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

partial class FreeLookCamera : FreeLookCameraBase
{
	public bool Enabled
	{
		set
		{
			_enabled = value;
			if (_enabled)
			{
				// save current active camera
				_previousCamera = GetViewport().GetCamera3D();
				_previousMouseMode = Input.MouseMode;

				if (_previousCamera != null)
				{
					// Copy current camera properties into this camera
					GlobalTransform = _previousCamera.GlobalTransform;
					Fov = _previousCamera.Fov;
					Near = _previousCamera.Near;
					Far = _previousCamera.Far;
					Projection = _previousCamera.Projection;

					// disable current camera
					_previousCamera.Current = false;
				}

				// Enable free look camera and set as the current one
				Current = true;
			}
			else
			{
				Current = false;
				if (_previousCamera != null)
				{
					_previousCamera.Current = true;
					Input.MouseMode = _previousMouseMode;
				}
			}
		}

		get
		{
			return _enabled;
		}
	}

	private bool _enabled = false;
	private Camera3D _previousCamera;
	private Input.MouseModeEnum _previousMouseMode;

	public override void _Ready()
	{
		Current = false;
	}
	public override void _Input(InputEvent _event)
	{
		if (!Enabled)
		{
			return;
		}
		base._Input(_event);
	}
	public override void _Process(double delta)
	{
		if (!Enabled)
		{
			return;
		}

		base._Process(delta);
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/FreeLookCameraBase.cs
```
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

partial class FreeLookCameraBase : Camera3D
{
	// This is a translation from the original free camera found in AssetStore 

	// Modifier keys' speed multiplier
	const float SHIFT_MULTIPLIER = 2.5f;
	const float ALT_MULTIPLIER = 1.0f / SHIFT_MULTIPLIER;

	[Export(PropertyHint.Range, "0.0f,1.0f")]
	public float sensitivity = 0.25f;

	// Mouse state
	private Vector2 _mouse_position = new Vector2(0.0f, 0.0f);
	private float _total_pitch = 0.0f;

	// Movement state
	private Vector3 _direction = new Vector3(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
	private Vector3 _velocity = new Vector3(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
	private float _acceleration = 30f;
	private float _deceleration = -10f;
	private float _vel_multiplier = 15f;

	// Keyboard state
	private bool _w = false;
	private bool _s = false;
	private bool _a = false;
	private bool _d = false;
	private bool _q = false;
	private bool _e = false;
	private bool _shift = false;
	private bool _alt = false;

	public override void _Input(InputEvent _event)
	{
		// Receives mouse motion
		InputEventMouseMotion mouseMotionEvent = _event as InputEventMouseMotion;
		if (mouseMotionEvent != null)
		{
			_mouse_position = mouseMotionEvent.Relative;
		}

		// Receives mouse button input
		InputEventMouseButton mouseButtonEvent = _event as InputEventMouseButton;
		if (mouseButtonEvent != null)
		{
			switch (mouseButtonEvent.ButtonIndex)
			{
				case MouseButton.Right: // Only allows rotation if right click down
					{
						Input.MouseMode = mouseButtonEvent.Pressed ? Input.MouseModeEnum.Captured : Input.MouseModeEnum.Visible;
					}
					break;

				case MouseButton.WheelUp: // Increases max velocity
					{
						_vel_multiplier = Mathf.Clamp(_vel_multiplier * 1.1f, 0.2f, 20f);
					}
					break;

				case MouseButton.WheelDown: // Decreases max velocity
					{
						_vel_multiplier = Mathf.Clamp(_vel_multiplier / 1.1f, 0.2f, 20f);
					}
					break;
			}
		}

		// Receives key input
		InputEventKey keyEvent = _event as InputEventKey;
		if (keyEvent != null)
		{
			switch (keyEvent.Keycode)
			{
				case Key.W:
					{
						_w = keyEvent.Pressed;
					}
					break;

				case Key.S:
					{
						_s = keyEvent.Pressed;
					}
					break;

				case Key.A:
					{
						_a = keyEvent.Pressed;
					}
					break;

				case Key.D:
					{
						_d = keyEvent.Pressed;
					}
					break;

				case Key.Q:
					{
						_q = keyEvent.Pressed;
					}
					break;

				case Key.E:
					{
						_e = keyEvent.Pressed;
					}
					break;
			}
		}
	}

	// Updates mouselook and movement every frame
	public override void _Process(double delta)
	{
		_update_mouselook();
		_update_movement((float)delta);
	}

	// Updates camera movement
	private void _update_movement(float delta)
	{
		// Computes desired direction from key states
		_direction = Vector3.Zero;
		if (_d) _direction.X += 1.0f;
		if (_a) _direction.X -= 1.0f;
		if (_e) _direction.Y += 1.0f;
		if (_q) _direction.Y -= 1.0f;
		if (_s) _direction.Z += 1.0f;
		if (_w) _direction.Z -= 1.0f;

		// Computes the change in velocity due to desired direction and "drag"
		// The "drag" is a constant acceleration on the camera to bring it's velocity to 0
		Vector3 offset = _direction.Normalized() * _acceleration * _vel_multiplier * delta
					   + _velocity.Normalized() * _deceleration * _vel_multiplier * delta;

		// Compute modifiers' speed multiplier
		float speed_multi = 1.0f;
		if (_shift) speed_multi *= SHIFT_MULTIPLIER;
		if (_alt) speed_multi *= ALT_MULTIPLIER;

		// Checks if we should bother translating the camera
		if ((_direction == Vector3.Zero) && (offset.LengthSquared() > _velocity.LengthSquared()))
		{
			// Sets the velocity to 0 to prevent jittering due to imperfect deceleration
			_velocity = Vector3.Zero;
		}
		else
		{
			// Clamps speed to stay within maximum value (_vel_multiplier)
			_velocity.X = Mathf.Clamp(_velocity.X + offset.X, -_vel_multiplier, _vel_multiplier);
			_velocity.Y = Mathf.Clamp(_velocity.Y + offset.Y, -_vel_multiplier, _vel_multiplier);
			_velocity.Z = Mathf.Clamp(_velocity.Z + offset.Z, -_vel_multiplier, _vel_multiplier);

			Translate(_velocity * delta * speed_multi);
		}
	}

	// Updates mouse look
	private void _update_mouselook()
	{
		// Only rotates mouse if the mouse is 
		if (Input.MouseMode == Input.MouseModeEnum.Captured)
		{
			_mouse_position *= sensitivity;
			float yaw = _mouse_position.X;
			float pitch = _mouse_position.Y;
			_mouse_position = Vector2.Zero;

			// Prevents looking up/down too far
			pitch = Mathf.Clamp(pitch, -90 - _total_pitch, 90 - _total_pitch);
			_total_pitch += pitch;

			RotateY(Mathf.DegToRad(-yaw));
			RotateObjectLocal(new Vector3(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f), Mathf.DegToRad(-pitch));
		}
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Root.cs
```
using System;
using System.Buffers.Binary;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using BenVoxel.FileToVoxCore;
using BenVoxel.Models;
using BenVoxel.VR.Props;
using BenVoxel.VR.Tools;
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

/// <summary>
/// Application root. Everything in the scene is created here in code; root.tscn contains only this node.
///
/// First-pass demo: loads Sora.vox, uploads it to the GPU through <see cref="VoxelBridge"/> and renders it
/// by raymarching inside a bounding box (see voxel_raymarch.gdshader), in VR if OpenXR is available and
/// otherwise on the flat screen with a free-look camera (also forced by the --flatscreen argument).
///
/// Coordinate systems: voxel data is Z-up (MagicaVoxel); Godot is Y-up. Everything on the C# side that
/// positions the box converts (x, y, z)_zup to (x, z, y)_yup. The shader converts back when it reads
/// voxels, so "voxel space" is Godot-oriented with one unit per voxel. The model is placed in the world
/// by the transform of <see cref="_modelPivot"/>, which maps voxel space to metres (see
/// <see cref="MetresPerVoxel"/>); the shader follows that transform through inv_model_matrix.
/// </summary>
public partial class Root : Node3D
{
	/// <summary>World size of one voxel in metres. 0.01 makes Sora about the size of a figurine.</summary>
	private const float MetresPerVoxel = 0.01f;
	/// <summary>Where the centre of the model sits relative to the floor-level origin, in metres: in front of the player at chest height.</summary>
	private static readonly Vector3 ModelWorldCenter = new(0f, 1.2f, -0.6f);

	private bool _vrActive;
	private XROrigin3D _xrOrigin;
	private XRCamera3D _xrCamera;
	/// <summary>Both hands and everything they can touch; null on the flat screen.</summary>
	private InteractionManager _interaction;
	private FreeLookCamera _flatCamera;

	/// <summary>Seconds per blink phase of the edit cursor.</summary>
	private const double CursorBlinkPeriod = 0.4;
	/// <summary>One-voxel translucent cube, child of <see cref="_modelPivot"/> and snapped to the voxel under the right controller's tip.</summary>
	private MeshInstance3D _cursor;
	private StandardMaterial3D _cursorMaterial;
	private double _cursorTime;

	/// <summary>World position of the ortho-sprite preview's centre when the demo starts: to the left of the model.</summary>
	private static readonly Vector3 SpriteWorldCenter = ModelWorldCenter + new Vector3(-0.4f, 0f, 0f);
	/// <summary>Non-editable ortho-sprite preview of the same model; also a grab target.</summary>
	private VoxelOrthoSprite _sprite;
	/// <summary>Model bounds in voxel space (Y-up), kept for the sprite and for grab hit-testing.</summary>
	private Vector3 _boxCenter;
	private Vector3 _boxSize;
	/// <summary>Where a summoned toy box lands: this far beyond the hand, and this far below it, in metres.</summary>
	private const float ToyBoxSummonDistance = 0.12f;
	private const float ToyBoxSummonDrop = 0.05f;
	/// <summary>Null until first summoned.</summary>
	private ToyBox _toyBox;
	/// <summary>The point light, and what a trigger on it does. Null until a way to spawn it exists, so for now the viewer's head lights the model.</summary>
	private LightProp _light;
	/// <summary>The tool menu, opened by clicking either thumbstick; null on the flat screen.</summary>
	private RadialMenu _menu;
	private EditTool _tool = EditTool.Pencil;

	/// <summary>Uploads the model's segments to GPU textures and keeps them in sync with edits.</summary>
	private VoxelBridge _voxelBridge;
	/// <summary>Material running voxel_raymarch.gdshader; carries every shader uniform.</summary>
	private ShaderMaterial _raymarchMaterial;
	/// <summary>256x1 RGBA8 palette lookup texture bound to the shader.</summary>
	private ImageTexture _paletteTexture;

	private SegmentedBrickModel _model;
	/// <summary>256 palette colours, each a uint packed as 0xRRGGBBAA ("rgba8888, big endian", see VoxFileModel.Argb2rgba). Entry 0 is empty.</summary>
	private uint[] _palette;

	/// <summary>Maps voxel space to world space: scale is <see cref="MetresPerVoxel"/>, position puts the model centre at <see cref="ModelWorldCenter"/>.</summary>
	private VoxelModelProp _modelPivot;
	/// <summary>
	/// Box mesh instance covering the model's bounds, child of <see cref="_modelPivot"/>. It is only the
	/// rasterisation footprint: the shader ignores its geometry and traces rays through the voxels inside it.
	/// </summary>
	private MeshInstance3D _boundingBox;
	private BoxMesh _boxMesh;

	/// <summary>Palette index written by the pencil. Index 0 is empty, so this must be 1 to 255.</summary>
	private byte _paintColor = 1;
	/// <summary>Last segment count printed, so the log only reports changes.</summary>
	private int _lastSegmentCount;

	public override void _Ready()
	{
		SetupDisplay();
		LoadModel();
		SetupRaymarchBox();
		SetupInteractables();
		SetupMenu();
		SetupShaderUniforms();
		// Subscribe to model changes to update shader when data changes
		_model.OnSegmentLoaded += _ => UpdateShaderSegments();
		_model.OnSegmentUnloaded += _ => UpdateShaderSegments();
		_model.OnBrickDirty += (_, _, _) => UpdateShaderSegments();
		_model.OnBoundsChanged += HandleBoundsChanged;
		GD.Print(_vrActive
			? "Ready in VR. Right trigger adds voxels at the cursor, A removes them."
			: "Ready on the flat screen. Right-click + WASDQE to fly around.");
	}

	#region Display

	/// <summary>
	/// Picks VR or flat screen. VR is used when the OpenXR interface initialises; otherwise (or with
	/// --flatscreen / --no-vr) a free-look camera is used so the shader can still be tested at a desk.
	/// </summary>
	private void SetupDisplay()
	{
		RenderingServer.SetDefaultClearColor(new Color(0.1f, 0.1f, 0.12f));
		// Arguments after "--" are only in the user args, so check both lists
		static bool IsFlatArg(string a) => a is "--flatscreen" or "--no-vr";
		bool forceFlat = Array.Exists(OS.GetCmdlineArgs(), IsFlatArg) || Array.Exists(OS.GetCmdlineUserArgs(), IsFlatArg);
		XRInterface xr = forceFlat ? null : XRServer.FindInterface("OpenXR");
		if (xr is not null && xr.Initialize())
		{
			GD.Print("OpenXR initialized successfully");
			// The headset display paces the frame loop; desktop vsync would only add latency.
			DisplayServer.WindowSetVsyncMode(DisplayServer.VSyncMode.Disabled);
			GetViewport().UseXR = true;
			_vrActive = true;
			EnablePassthrough(xr);
			SetupXRRig();
		}
		else
		{
			GD.Print(forceFlat
				? "Flatscreen mode forced"
				: "OpenXR not available, using flatscreen mode");
			SetupFlatCamera();
		}
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Shows the real world behind the model if the runtime supports it, otherwise leaves the dark
	/// background. Two routes: the vendor passthrough API (Meta, via the OpenXR vendors plugin), or the
	/// standard OpenXR alpha-blend environment mode. Either way the viewport background must be
	/// transparent so the empty pixels are left for the compositor to fill with the camera image. The
	/// raymarch shader discards misses and outputs opaque hits, so the model itself is unaffected.
	/// </summary>
	private void EnablePassthrough(XRInterface xr)
	{
		bool enabled = false;
		if (xr.IsPassthroughSupported())
			enabled = xr.StartPassthrough();
		if (!enabled && xr.GetSupportedEnvironmentBlendModes().Contains((int)XRInterface.EnvironmentBlendModeEnum.AlphaBlend))
		{
			xr.EnvironmentBlendMode = XRInterface.EnvironmentBlendModeEnum.AlphaBlend;
			enabled = true;
		}
		GetViewport().TransparentBg = enabled;
		GD.Print(enabled ? "Passthrough enabled" : "Passthrough not available; using opaque background");
	}

	/// <summary>Builds the tracked rig: origin at floor level, head camera, and both controllers with small marker cubes, and the interaction manager that reads them.</summary>
	private void SetupXRRig()
	{
		_xrOrigin = new XROrigin3D { Name = "XROrigin" };
		_xrCamera = new XRCamera3D { Name = "XRCamera", Current = true };
		_xrOrigin.AddChild(_xrCamera);
		XRController3D leftController = CreateController("LeftController", "left_hand");
		XRController3D rightController = CreateController("RightController", "right_hand");
		_xrOrigin.AddChild(leftController);
		_xrOrigin.AddChild(rightController);
		AddChild(_xrOrigin);
		_interaction = new InteractionManager(new Hand(leftController), new Hand(rightController));
	}

	private static XRController3D CreateController(string name, string tracker)
	{
		XRController3D controller = new()
		{
			Name = name,
			Tracker = tracker,
			Pose = "grip",
		};
		controller.AddChild(new MeshInstance3D
		{
			Name = "Marker",
			Mesh = new BoxMesh { Size = new Vector3(0.04f, 0.04f, 0.1f) },
			MaterialOverride = new StandardMaterial3D
			{
				ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded,
				AlbedoColor = new Color(0.7f, 0.7f, 0.75f),
			},
		});
		return controller;
	}

	/// <summary>Desktop fallback: free-look camera a metre from the model, looking at it.</summary>
	private void SetupFlatCamera()
	{
		_flatCamera = new FreeLookCamera
		{
			Name = "FlatCamera",
			Position = ModelWorldCenter + new Vector3(0f, 0.2f, 1.0f),
		};
		AddChild(_flatCamera);
		_flatCamera.LookAt(ModelWorldCenter);
		_flatCamera.Near = 0.02f;
		_flatCamera.Enabled = true; // after AddChild so it can find the previous camera
	}

	#endregion

	#region Model

	/// <summary>Loads the embedded Sora.vox and builds the segmented model and its GPU bridge.</summary>
	private void LoadModel()
	{
		GD.Print("Loading voxel model...");
		using System.IO.Stream stream = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream("Sora.vox")
			?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Embedded resource Sora.vox not found");
		VoxFileModel vox = new(stream);
		_palette = vox.Palette; // 256 RGBA8888 colors
		_model = new SegmentedBrickModel(vox);
		_voxelBridge = new(_model);
		GD.Print($"Model size: ({_model.SizeX}, {_model.SizeY}, {_model.SizeZ}), active segments: {_voxelBridge.ActiveSegmentCount}");
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Creates the pivot and the bounding box mesh sized to the model's bounds and assigns the raymarch
	/// shader material. The BoxMesh is centred on its own origin, so the instance's Position is the box
	/// centre in voxel space, which is why the shader adds box_center to mesh-local coordinates.
	/// </summary>
	private void SetupRaymarchBox()
	{
		(Vector3 center, Vector3 size) = CalculateBoundingBox(_model.CurrentBounds);
		GD.Print($"Voxel-space bounds (Y-up): center={center}, size={size}");
		_modelPivot = new VoxelModelProp
		{
			Scale = Vector3.One * MetresPerVoxel,
			// Put the model's centre at ModelWorldCenter
			Position = ModelWorldCenter - center * MetresPerVoxel,
		};
		AddChild(_modelPivot);
		SetupCursor();
		_boxMesh = new BoxMesh { Size = size };
		_raymarchMaterial = new ShaderMaterial { Shader = GD.Load<Shader>("res://voxel_raymarch.gdshader") };
		_boundingBox = new MeshInstance3D
		{
			Name = "VoxelBox",
			Mesh = _boxMesh,
			Position = center,
			MaterialOverride = _raymarchMaterial,
			// This is a raymarched volume, not geometry
			CastShadow = GeometryInstance3D.ShadowCastingSetting.Off,
		};
		_modelPivot.AddChild(_boundingBox);
		SetBoxUniforms(center, size);
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Creates the ortho-sprite preview. Its node maps voxel space to metres like the model pivot, and it is
	/// positioned so the model's centre lands on <see cref="SpriteWorldCenter"/>.
	/// </summary>
	private void SetupSprite()
	{
		_sprite = new VoxelOrthoSprite
		{
			Scale = Vector3.One * MetresPerVoxel,
			Position = SpriteWorldCenter - _boxCenter * MetresPerVoxel,
		};
		AddProp(_sprite);
		SetBoxUniforms(_boxCenter, _boxSize);
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Creates the edit cursor: a translucent one-voxel cube that will preview where a voxel would be added
	/// or removed. It is ordinary geometry in the transparent pass, which runs after the opaque pass where the
	/// raymarch shader has written DEPTH at each hit voxel, so the depth test against the model just works.
	/// Being a child of the pivot it is one voxel in size at any grab scale.
	/// </summary>
	private void SetupCursor()
	{
		_cursorMaterial = new StandardMaterial3D
		{
			ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded,
			Transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TransparencyEnum.Alpha,
			AlbedoColor = new Color(1f, 0.9f, 0.2f, 0.45f),
			CullMode = BaseMaterial3D.CullModeEnum.Disabled,
		};
		_cursor = new MeshInstance3D
		{
			Name = "EditCursor",
			Mesh = new BoxMesh { Size = Vector3.One * 1.02f }, // a hair oversize so it does not z-fight voxel faces
			MaterialOverride = _cursorMaterial,
			CastShadow = GeometryInstance3D.ShadowCastingSetting.Off,
			Visible = false, // until a controller exists
		};
		_modelPivot.AddChild(_cursor);
	}

	/// <summary>Puts a prop in the world and makes it grabbable. This is all a new prop needs.</summary>
	private void AddProp(Prop prop)
	{
		AddChild(prop);
		_interaction?.Register(prop);
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Registers what the hands can touch from the start: the model, which is also what a grab that touches nothing carries.
	/// </summary>
	private void SetupInteractables()
	{
		if (_interaction is null)
			return;
		_interaction.Register(_modelPivot, fallback: true);
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Fills the tool menu. Each entry is an icon and what choosing it does; the menu lays out any number of entries,
	/// so a new tool is one more line here.
	/// </summary>
	private void SetupMenu()
	{
		if (_interaction is null)
			return;
		_menu = new RadialMenu { Name = "ToolMenu" };
		AddChild(_menu);
		_menu.AddItem("Pencil", new ModelProp("res://assets/pencil/pencil.glb", 1f), _ => _tool = EditTool.Pencil, tiltDegrees: 45f);
		_menu.AddItem("Select", new WireframeCube(), _ => _tool = EditTool.Select);
		_menu.AddItem("Toybox", new ToyBox(), SummonToyBox);
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Fills the toy box. Each entry is an icon, the kind of grab its prop takes, and how to bring the prop into the
	/// world under a hand and take it out again; the box lays out any number of entries, so a new prop is one more call.
	/// </summary>
	private void SetupToyBoxItems(ToyBox box)
	{
		box.AddItem("Light", () => MiniIcon.LightSphere(LightProp.Radius), GrabMode.Drag, SpawnLight, DespawnLight);
		// The pencil stands in for props that will have real functions
		box.AddItem("Pencil", () => new ModelProp("res://assets/pencil/pencil.glb", 1f), GrabMode.Free, SpawnPencil, DespawnProp, tiltDegrees: 45f);
	}

	/// <summary>There is only one light; asking for it again brings it to the hand.</summary>
	private IInteractable SpawnLight(Hand hand)
	{
		if (_light is null)
		{
			_light = new LightProp(hand.TipWorld, CellTouchesSphere);
			AddChild(_light.Node);
			_interaction.Register(_light);
		}
		_light.Node.GlobalPosition = hand.TipWorld;
		return _light;
	}

	private void DespawnLight(IInteractable light)
	{
		_interaction.Unregister(light);
		light.Node.QueueFree();
		_light = null;
	}

	private IInteractable SpawnPencil(Hand hand)
	{
		ModelProp pencil = new("res://assets/pencil/pencil.glb", 0.2f);
		AddProp(pencil);
		pencil.GlobalPosition = hand.TipWorld;
		return pencil;
	}

	private void DespawnProp(IInteractable prop)
	{
		_interaction.Unregister(prop);
		prop.Node.QueueFree();
	}

	/// <summary>Moves the toy box to just beyond a hand, as seen by the viewer, turned to face them. It is created the first time.</summary>
	private void SummonToyBox(Hand hand)
	{
		Vector3 head = GetHeadWorldPosition();
		Vector3 away = hand.TipWorld - head;
		away.Y = 0f;
		away = away.LengthSquared() > 1e-4f ? away.Normalized() : Vector3.Forward;
		Vector3 position = hand.TipWorld + away * ToyBoxSummonDistance;
		position.Y = hand.TipWorld.Y - ToyBoxSummonDrop; // the chest's origin is the centre of its base
		if (_toyBox is null)
		{
			_toyBox = new ToyBox { Interaction = _interaction };
			SetupToyBoxItems(_toyBox);
			AddProp(_toyBox);
			_interaction.Released += _toyBox.OnReleased;
		}
		_toyBox.GlobalPosition = position;
		// The chest's front is +Z
		_toyBox.GlobalRotation = new Vector3(0f, Mathf.Atan2(head.X - position.X, head.Z - position.Z), 0f);
	}

	/// <summary>The centre, in voxel space, of the voxel containing a world point.</summary>
	private Vector3 SnapToVoxel(Vector3 world) => _modelPivot.ToLocal(world).Floor() + Vector3.One * 0.5f;

	/// <summary>True if the voxel containing <paramref name="tip"/> overlaps a sphere. Tested in voxel space, where the voxel is an axis-aligned unit cube.</summary>
	private bool CellTouchesSphere(Vector3 tip, Vector3 sphereWorld, float radiusMetres)
	{
		Vector3 cell = SnapToVoxel(tip);
		Vector3 centre = _modelPivot.ToLocal(sphereWorld);
		float radius = radiusMetres / _modelPivot.GlobalTransform.Basis.Scale.X;
		Vector3 half = Vector3.One * 0.5f;
		Vector3 closest = centre.Clamp(cell - half, cell + half);
		return closest.DistanceSquaredTo(centre) <= radius * radius;
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Where the shader's light is in world space: the sphere when it exists and is on, otherwise the viewer's head (the
	/// XR camera is the midpoint between the eyes; on the flat screen it is the free-look camera).
	/// </summary>
	private Vector3 GetLightWorldPosition() => _light is { On: true } ? _light.WorldPosition : GetHeadWorldPosition();

	/// <summary>The viewer's position: the XR camera (midpoint between the eyes) in VR, otherwise the free-look camera.</summary>
	private Vector3 GetHeadWorldPosition() => (_vrActive ? _xrCamera : (Node3D)_flatCamera).GlobalPosition;

	/// <summary>
	/// Snaps the cursor to the voxel containing the right controller's tip and blinks it between two
	/// looks: depth-tested (parts behind the model are hidden) and drawn over everything (x-ray), so
	/// you can tell both where it is and whether the model is in front of it.
	/// </summary>
	private void UpdateCursor(double delta)
	{
		if (_cursor is null || _interaction is null)
			return;
		Hand hand = _interaction.Primary;
		_cursor.Position = SnapToVoxel(hand.TipWorld);
		// Over the light the cursor gives way to the halo, and it only belongs to the pencil, which the menu replaces while open
		_cursor.Visible = hand.HasTracking && _tool == EditTool.Pencil && _menu is not { IsOpen: true }
			&& hand.Hovered is not { SuppressesEditCursor: true };
		_cursorTime += delta;
		_cursorMaterial.NoDepthTest = (int)(_cursorTime / CursorBlinkPeriod) % 2 == 1;
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// While the edit cursor is showing, the primary hand's trigger writes <see cref="_paintColor"/> into the voxel
	/// under it and A clears it. Both act every frame they are held, so sweeping the hand draws a line. A write that
	/// would not change the voxel is skipped, because every change re-uploads the whole segment to the GPU.
	/// </summary>
	private void ApplyPencil()
	{
		if (_cursor is not { Visible: true })
			return;
		Hand hand = _interaction.Primary;
		bool add = hand.TriggerPressed, remove = hand.AxPressed;
		if (add == remove)
			return; // neither, or both
		// Voxel space is Y-up, storage is Z-up: (x, y, z)_yup is (x, z, y)_zup
		Vector3 cell = (_cursor.Position - Vector3.One * 0.5f).Round();
		if (cell.X < 0 || cell.Y < 0 || cell.Z < 0 || cell.X > ushort.MaxValue || cell.Y > ushort.MaxValue || cell.Z > ushort.MaxValue)
			return;
		ushort x = (ushort)cell.X, y = (ushort)cell.Z, z = (ushort)cell.Y;
		byte value = add ? _paintColor : (byte)0;
		if (_model[x, y, z] == value)
			return;
		_model[x, y, z] = value;
		hand.Pulse(0.2f, 0.02f);
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Calculates bounding box center and size in voxel space (Y-up) from model bounds (Z-up).
	/// The max is extended by 2 voxels because bounds are stored at brick granularity (2x2x2 voxels), so
	/// the last brick may contain voxels up to max + 1. Corners are therefore always integer coordinates.
	/// Returns a zero-sized box at the origin if the model has no bounds yet.
	/// </summary>
	private static (Vector3 Center, Vector3 Size) CalculateBoundingBox(SegmentedBrickModel.Bounds? bounds)
	{
		if (!bounds.HasValue)
			return (Vector3.Zero, Vector3.Zero);
		// (X, Y, Z)_zup -> (X, Z, Y)_yup
		SegmentedBrickModel.Bounds b = bounds.Value;
		Vector3 min = new(b.MinX, b.MinZ, b.MinY);
		Vector3 max = new(b.MaxX + 2, b.MaxZ + 2, b.MaxY + 2);
		return ((min + max) / 2, max - min);
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Handles bounds changes (growth from edits, or shrinkage after GPU trimming) by resizing and
	/// repositioning the raymarching bounding box and updating the shader's copy of it. The pivot is
	/// left alone so the voxels do not move in the world when the bounds change.
	/// </summary>
	private void HandleBoundsChanged(SegmentedBrickModel.Bounds? oldBounds, SegmentedBrickModel.Bounds newBounds)
	{
		if (_boundingBox is null || _boxMesh is null)
			return;
		(Vector3 center, Vector3 size) = CalculateBoundingBox(newBounds);
		_boxMesh.Size = size;
		_boundingBox.Position = center;
		SetBoxUniforms(center, size);
		GD.Print($"Bounds changed: {oldBounds} -> {newBounds}");
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Tells the shader the bounding box in voxel space; it intersects rays with this box analytically
	/// to find where each ray enters the volume. Must be called whenever the BoxMesh size or the
	/// instance Position changes, or rays will start in the wrong place.
	/// </summary>
	private void SetBoxUniforms(Vector3 center, Vector3 size)
	{
		_boxCenter = center;
		_boxSize = size;
		_modelPivot.BoxCenter = center;
		_modelPivot.BoxSize = size;
		if (_sprite is not null)
		{
			_sprite.BoxCenter = center;
			_sprite.BoxSize = size;
		}
		SetSharedParameter("box_center", center);
		SetSharedParameter("box_size", size);
	}

	#endregion

	#region Shader uniforms

	/// <summary>Sets a uniform on both voxel materials: the editable raymarcher and the ortho sprite. Both read the same voxel data.</summary>
	private void SetSharedParameter(StringName name, Variant value)
	{
		_raymarchMaterial?.SetShaderParameter(name, value);
		_sprite?.Material.SetShaderParameter(name, value);
	}

	/// <summary>Binds the segment count, directory texture, per-segment voxel textures and palette to the shader.</summary>
	private void SetupShaderUniforms()
	{
		UpdateShaderSegments();
		SetupPaletteTexture();
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Updates shader uniforms when segments are added or removed (also called from the model's
	/// segment and brick events). The voxel textures are bound as an array in directory order, so the
	/// directory's texture index for a segment is its position in that array. The array holds at most
	/// <see cref="VoxelBridge.MaxSegments"/> textures (a temporary limit; see the note on that constant).
	/// </summary>
	private void UpdateShaderSegments()
	{
		if (_raymarchMaterial is null || _voxelBridge is null)
			return;
		SetSharedParameter("active_segment_count", _voxelBridge.ActiveSegmentCount);
		if (_voxelBridge.DirectoryTexture is not null)
			SetSharedParameter("segment_directory", _voxelBridge.DirectoryTexture);
		Godot.Collections.Array<Texture3D> textureArray = [];
		foreach (VoxelBridge.SegmentEntry entry in _voxelBridge.Directory)
		{
			uint segmentId = ((uint)entry.X << 18) | ((uint)entry.Y << 9) | entry.Z;
			if (_voxelBridge.Textures.TryGetValue(segmentId, out ImageTexture3D texture))
				textureArray.Add(texture);
			else
				GD.PrintErr($"Texture not found for segment ({entry.X}, {entry.Y}, {entry.Z})");
		}
		SetSharedParameter("segment_voxels", textureArray);
		if (_voxelBridge.ActiveSegmentCount != _lastSegmentCount)
		{
			_lastSegmentCount = _voxelBridge.ActiveSegmentCount;
			GD.Print($"Shader updated: {_voxelBridge.ActiveSegmentCount} segments");
		}
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Builds the 256x1 RGBA8 palette texture. The model stores each colour as a 0xRRGGBBAA uint.
	/// Reversing the endianness makes it 0xAABBGGRR, whose little-endian bytes in memory are R, G, B, A,
	/// which is the byte order an RGBA8 image expects; the raw byte copy below relies on that, so the
	/// two steps must be changed together. The shader reads this texture as sRGB (source_color).
	/// </summary>
	private void SetupPaletteTexture()
	{
		uint[] converted = new uint[256];
		for (int i = 0; i < 256; i++)
			converted[i] = BinaryPrimitives.ReverseEndianness(_palette[i]);
		byte[] paletteData = MemoryMarshal.AsBytes(converted.AsSpan()).ToArray();
		_paletteTexture = ImageTexture.CreateFromImage(Image.CreateFromData(256, 1, false, Image.Format.Rgba8, paletteData));
		SetSharedParameter("palette", _paletteTexture);
	}

	#endregion

	/// <summary>
	/// Per-frame work: lets the bridge finish any pending GPU bounds trim, refreshes the inverse model
	/// matrix the shader needs, and applies the pencil.
	/// </summary>
	public override void _Process(double delta)
	{
		_voxelBridge?.Poll();
		_interaction?.Update();
		if (_menu is not null)
		{
			Vector3 head = GetHeadWorldPosition();
			_menu.Update(_interaction.Left, head);
			_menu.Update(_interaction.Right, head);
		}
		_toyBox?.Face(GetHeadWorldPosition());
		UpdateCursor(delta);
		ApplyPencil();
		// The fragment stage has no inverse of MODEL_MATRIX, so supply it every frame. Reading it from
		// the box's GlobalTransform includes the pivot, so moving or scaling the pivot just works.
		_raymarchMaterial?.SetShaderParameter("inv_model_matrix", new Projection(_boundingBox.GlobalTransform.AffineInverse()));
		Vector3 lightWorld = GetLightWorldPosition();
		_raymarchMaterial?.SetShaderParameter("light_position", _modelPivot.ToLocal(lightWorld));
		_sprite?.UpdateView(GetHeadWorldPosition(), lightWorld);
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Screenshot keys: F9 and Ctrl+Shift+S. Steam's own F12 only works for apps it launched, and it may take other
	/// function keys too, hence the second one.
	/// </summary>
	public override void _UnhandledKeyInput(InputEvent @event)
	{
		if (@event is InputEventKey { Pressed: true, Echo: false } key
			&& (key.Keycode == Key.F9 || (key.Keycode == Key.S && key.CtrlPressed && key.ShiftPressed)))
			SaveScreenshot();
	}

	/// <summary>Offscreen viewport and camera for screenshots; null until the first one. The XR render target cannot be read back, so the scene is drawn again here from the head's pose.</summary>
	private SubViewport _shotViewport;
	private Camera3D _shotCamera;
	private bool _shotBusy;

	private const int ScreenshotWidth = 1920;
	private const int ScreenshotHeight = 1080;
	private const float ScreenshotFov = 90f;

	/// <summary>
	/// Renders the scene once from the viewer's head with a flat camera and saves it as a PNG in the user data folder,
	/// logging the path or the failure. It shows what the head sees, not the exact stereo image in the headset.
	/// </summary>
	private async void SaveScreenshot()
	{
		if (_shotBusy)
			return;
		_shotBusy = true;
		if (_shotViewport is null)
		{
			_shotViewport = new SubViewport
			{
				Name = "ScreenshotViewport",
				Size = new Vector2I(ScreenshotWidth, ScreenshotHeight),
				RenderTargetUpdateMode = SubViewport.UpdateMode.Disabled,
			};
			_shotCamera = new Camera3D { Name = "ScreenshotCamera", Fov = ScreenshotFov, Near = 0.02f, Current = true };
			_shotViewport.AddChild(_shotCamera);
			AddChild(_shotViewport);
		}
		_shotCamera.GlobalTransform = (_vrActive ? _xrCamera : (Node3D)_flatCamera).GlobalTransform;
		_shotViewport.RenderTargetUpdateMode = SubViewport.UpdateMode.Once;
		await ToSignal(RenderingServer.Singleton, RenderingServerInstance.SignalName.FramePostDraw);
		string path = $"user://screenshot_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd_HHmmss}.png";
		Error error = _shotViewport.GetTexture().GetImage().SavePng(path);
		GD.Print(error == Error.Ok
			? $"Screenshot saved: {ProjectSettings.GlobalizePath(path)}"
			: $"Screenshot failed ({error}): {path}");
		_shotBusy = false;
	}

	public override void _ExitTree() => _voxelBridge?.Dispose();
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/VoxelBridge.cs
```
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using BenVoxel.Models;
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

/// <summary>
/// Bridges SegmentedBrickModel (CPU) to Godot GPU textures for raymarching.
///
/// The model is split into segments of 128³ voxels. Each loaded segment becomes one 128³ 3D texture in
/// R8 format: one texel is one voxel, and its byte is the voxel's palette index. R8 is unsigned-normalised
/// (not an integer format), so the shader reads it with texelFetch and recovers the index as
/// round(value * 255), which is exact. The CPU model stores 2×2×2 bricks of 8 bytes; they are unpacked
/// into this linear layout on upload. Each texture is 128³ × 1 byte = 2 MB.
///
/// A small directory texture tells the shader which segments exist. It is rebuilt whenever a segment
/// loads or unloads.
///
/// What the shader (voxel_raymarch.gdshader) expects:
/// - uniform int active_segment_count         = ActiveSegmentCount
/// - uniform sampler2D segment_directory      = DirectoryTexture  (MaxSegments×1 RGBAF;
///                                              texel i = segX, segY, segZ, texture slot, as floats)
/// - uniform sampler3D segment_voxels[64]     = the entries of Textures, ordered as Directory is, so a
///                                              directory entry's TextureIndex is its array position
///
/// Voxel coordinates here are Z-up storage coordinates (MagicaVoxel convention). A voxel at (x, y, z)
/// is in segment (x >> 7, y >> 7, z >> 7), at texel (x &amp; 127, y &amp; 127, z &amp; 127) of that segment's texture.
///
/// The bridge also tracks when removals may have left the model's bounds larger than necessary and
/// can recompute tight bounds on the GPU (see <see cref="Poll"/> and <see cref="TrimBounds"/>).
/// </summary>
public sealed class VoxelBridge : IDisposable
{
	public readonly record struct SegmentEntry(ushort X, ushort Y, ushort Z, uint TextureIndex);

	private const int VoxelsPerAxis = 128; // 128³ voxels per segment
	private const int BricksPerAxis = 64;  // 64³ bricks per segment (CPU storage)
	private const int TotalBricks = BricksPerAxis * BricksPerAxis * BricksPerAxis; // 262,144
	private const int BytesPerSlice = VoxelsPerAxis * VoxelsPerAxis; // 16 KB, one Z slice of R8 texels

	private readonly SegmentedBrickModel _model;

	/// <summary>
	/// SegmentId → GPU texture (ImageTexture3D)
	/// </summary>
	private readonly Dictionary<uint, ImageTexture3D> _textures = [];

	/// <summary>
	/// Dense GPU directory (rebuilt on load/unload)
	/// </summary>
	private readonly List<SegmentEntry> _directory = [];

	/// <summary>
	/// GPU directory texture for spatial shader access
	/// Each texel = one segment entry (segX, segY, segZ, texIndex)
	/// </summary>
	private ImageTexture _directoryTexture;

	/// <summary>
	/// Maximum number of segments resident on the GPU at once. Must equal the size of the
	/// <c>segment_voxels</c> sampler3D array in voxel_raymarch.gdshader; if it is larger, the shader
	/// indexes past the end of the array. It also sizes the directory texture.
	///
	/// TEMPORARY LIMIT - NOT PART OF THE FORMAT. The file format addresses 512 segments per axis
	/// (SegmentedBrickModel.md), and each resident segment costs 2 MB of GPU memory here, so 64 is
	/// 128 MB. Supporting big scenes needs a different residency scheme, for example one large 3D atlas
	/// texture whose slots are paged in and out, with the directory mapping segment -> atlas slot. That
	/// would replace this constant, the per-segment ImageTexture3D dictionary, the sampler array in the
	/// shader, and the texture-array binding in Root.cs (SetupShaderUniforms / UpdateShaderSegments).
	/// </summary>
	public const int MaxSegments = 64;

	// GPU bounds computation
	private BoundsCompute _boundsCompute;
	private bool _boundsPotentiallyOversized;

	public VoxelBridge(SegmentedBrickModel model)
	{
		_model = model ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(model));

		_model.OnSegmentLoaded += HandleSegmentLoaded;
		_model.OnSegmentUnloaded += HandleSegmentUnloaded;
		_model.OnBrickDirty += HandleBrickDirty;

		// Initialize GPU bounds compute
		_boundsCompute = new BoundsCompute();

		// Upload any segments that were already loaded before we subscribed to events
		UploadExistingSegments();
	}

	private void UploadExistingSegments()
	{
		// Enumerate all existing bricks to find which segments exist
		HashSet<uint> seenSegments = new HashSet<uint>();
		foreach (BenVoxel.Structs.VoxelBrick brick in _model)
		{
			// Calculate segment ID from brick coordinates
			uint segmentId = ((uint)(brick.X >> 7) << 18) | ((uint)(brick.Y >> 7) << 9) | (uint)(brick.Z >> 7);

			if (seenSegments.Add(segmentId) && _model.TryGetSegment(segmentId, out ulong[] bricks))
				TryUploadSegment(segmentId, bricks);
		}

		if (seenSegments.Count > 0)
		{
			RebuildDirectory();
		}
	}

	public IReadOnlyList<SegmentEntry> Directory => _directory;
	public IReadOnlyDictionary<uint, ImageTexture3D> Textures => _textures;
	public ImageTexture DirectoryTexture => _directoryTexture;
	public int ActiveSegmentCount => _directory.Count;

	private void HandleSegmentLoaded(uint segmentId)
	{
		if (!_model.TryGetSegment(segmentId, out ulong[] bricks))
			return;

		if (TryUploadSegment(segmentId, bricks))
			RebuildDirectory();
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Uploads a segment's full texture (2 MB) unless doing so would exceed <see cref="MaxSegments"/>.
	/// Replacing a segment that is already resident is always allowed. A refused segment is NOT drawn:
	/// its voxels are treated as empty space, and an error is logged. Does not rebuild the directory.
	/// </summary>
	/// <returns>True if the segment is now resident on the GPU.</returns>
	private bool TryUploadSegment(uint segmentId, ulong[] bricks)
	{
		if (!_textures.ContainsKey(segmentId) && _textures.Count >= MaxSegments)
		{
			GD.PrintErr($"VoxelBridge: segment {segmentId} not uploaded; GPU limit of {MaxSegments} resident segments reached");
			return false;
		}
		_textures[segmentId] = CreateSegmentTexture(bricks);
		return true;
	}

	private void HandleBrickDirty(uint segmentId, int brickIndex, ulong payload)
	{
		if (!_textures.TryGetValue(segmentId, out ImageTexture3D texture))
			return; // Segment not resident on GPU

		// ImageTexture3D can only be updated whole, so the segment's texture is rewritten from the model.
		// Update keeps the same texture object, so nothing has to be rebound.
		if (_model.TryGetSegment(segmentId, out ulong[] bricks))
			texture.Update(CreateSliceImages(bricks));

		// If any voxels were cleared, bounds might need shrinking
		// We detect this by checking if the new payload has any zero bytes where it didn't before
		// For simplicity, mark as potentially oversized if payload is 0 or has any zero bytes
		if (payload == 0 || HasClearedVoxels(payload))
		{
			_boundsPotentiallyOversized = true;
		}
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Checks if a brick payload might have cleared voxels (contains zero bytes).
	/// </summary>
	private static bool HasClearedVoxels(ulong payload)
	{
		// Check each byte of the payload
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
		{
			if (((payload >> (i * 8)) & 0xFF) == 0)
				return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void HandleSegmentUnloaded(uint segmentId)
	{
		if (_textures.Remove(segmentId))
		{
			// ImageTexture3D will be garbage collected automatically
		}
		RebuildDirectory();
	}

	private void RebuildDirectory()
	{
		_directory.Clear();
		uint index = 0;
		foreach (uint segmentId in _textures.Keys)
			_directory.Add(new SegmentEntry(
				X: (ushort)((segmentId >> 18) & 0x1FF),
				Y: (ushort)((segmentId >> 9) & 0x1FF),
				Z: (ushort)(segmentId & 0x1FF),
				TextureIndex: index++));

		// Upload directory to GPU storage buffer
		UploadDirectoryToGPU();
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Uploads segment directory to GPU texture for spatial shader access.
	/// Format: RGBAF (2D texture, MaxSegments×1)
	/// Each texel: (segX, segY, segZ, texIndex) as floats
	/// </summary>
	private void UploadDirectoryToGPU()
	{
		_directoryTexture = null;

		if (_directory.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}

		// Pack directory into RGBAF texture data
		// Each pixel = 16 bytes (4 floats)
		byte[] textureData = new byte[MaxSegments * 16];

		for (int i = 0; i < _directory.Count; i++)
		{
			SegmentEntry entry = _directory[i];
			int offset = i * 16;

			// RGBA channels: segmentX, segmentY, segmentZ, textureIndex (as floats)
			BitConverter.GetBytes((float)entry.X).CopyTo(textureData, offset + 0);  // R
			BitConverter.GetBytes((float)entry.Y).CopyTo(textureData, offset + 4);  // G
			BitConverter.GetBytes((float)entry.Z).CopyTo(textureData, offset + 8);  // B
			BitConverter.GetBytes((float)entry.TextureIndex).CopyTo(textureData, offset + 12); // A
		}

		// Fill remaining entries with zeros (empty segments)
		// Already zeroed by default allocation

		// Create directory texture using ImageTexture
		Image image = Image.CreateFromData(MaxSegments, 1, false, Image.Format.Rgbaf, textureData);
		_directoryTexture = ImageTexture.CreateFromImage(image);

		GD.Print($"Directory texture created, {_directory.Count} segments");
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Creates the 128³ R8 texture for a segment from its 64³ brick array.
	/// </summary>
	private ImageTexture3D CreateSegmentTexture(ulong[] bricks)
	{
		ImageTexture3D texture = new();
		texture.Create(Image.Format.R8, VoxelsPerAxis, VoxelsPerAxis, VoxelsPerAxis, false, CreateSliceImages(bricks));
		return texture;
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Unpacks bricks into one R8 image per Z slice, the form ImageTexture3D takes. Brick i sits at
	/// (i &amp; 63, (i >> 6) &amp; 63, i >> 12); within a brick, byte k is the voxel at offset
	/// (k &amp; 1, (k >> 1) &amp; 1, k >> 2). Empty bricks are skipped, since the buffer starts zeroed.
	/// </summary>
	private static Godot.Collections.Array<Image> CreateSliceImages(ulong[] bricks)
	{
		byte[] voxels = new byte[VoxelsPerAxis * BytesPerSlice];
		for (int i = 0; i < TotalBricks; i++)
		{
			ulong brick = bricks[i];
			if (brick == 0)
				continue;
			int x0 = (i & 0x3F) << 1, y0 = ((i >> 6) & 0x3F) << 1, z0 = (i >> 12) << 1;
			for (int k = 0; k < 8; k++)
			{
				int x = x0 + (k & 1), y = y0 + ((k >> 1) & 1), z = z0 + (k >> 2);
				voxels[z * BytesPerSlice + y * VoxelsPerAxis + x] = (byte)(brick >> (k * 8));
			}
		}

		Godot.Collections.Array<Image> images = [];
		for (int z = 0; z < VoxelsPerAxis; z++)
		{
			byte[] sliceData = new byte[BytesPerSlice];
			Array.Copy(voxels, z * BytesPerSlice, sliceData, 0, BytesPerSlice);
			images.Add(Image.CreateFromData(VoxelsPerAxis, VoxelsPerAxis, false, Image.Format.R8, sliceData));
		}
		return images;
	}

	#region Bounds Trimming

	/// <summary>
	/// Whether bounds might be larger than necessary due to voxel removals.
	/// </summary>
	public bool BoundsPotentiallyOversized => _boundsPotentiallyOversized;

	/// <summary>
	/// Call this every frame to check if bounds need trimming.
	/// Uses GPU compute with throttling (max once per 100ms).
	/// </summary>
	public void Poll()
	{
		if (!_boundsPotentiallyOversized)
			return;

		// Try to compute bounds (will be throttled if called too frequently)
		SegmentedBrickModel.Bounds? result = _boundsCompute?.ComputeBounds(_textures, _directory);

		if (result.HasValue)
		{
			_model.ApplyTrimmedBounds(result);
			_boundsPotentiallyOversized = false;
			GD.Print($"VoxelBridge: Bounds trimmed to {result}");
		}
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Forces an immediate bounds recalculation via GPU compute shader.
	/// Bypasses throttling. Use for save/export operations.
	/// </summary>
	public void TrimBounds()
	{
		if (_boundsCompute == null || !_boundsCompute.IsAvailable)
		{
			// Fallback to CPU recalculation
			_model.RecalculateBounds();
			_boundsPotentiallyOversized = false;
			GD.Print("VoxelBridge: Bounds trimmed via CPU fallback");
			return;
		}

		SegmentedBrickModel.Bounds? result = _boundsCompute.ComputeBounds(_textures, _directory, force: true);
		_model.ApplyTrimmedBounds(result);
		_boundsPotentiallyOversized = false;

		GD.Print($"VoxelBridge: Bounds trimmed to {result}");
	}

	#endregion

	public void Dispose()
	{
		_model.OnSegmentLoaded -= HandleSegmentLoaded;
		_model.OnSegmentUnloaded -= HandleSegmentUnloaded;
		_model.OnBrickDirty -= HandleBrickDirty;

		_boundsCompute?.Dispose();
		_boundsCompute = null;

		// ImageTexture3D and ImageTexture will be garbage collected automatically
		_textures.Clear();
		_directoryTexture = null;
		_directory.Clear();
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/bounds_reduction.glsl
```
#[compute]
#version 450

// Workgroup size: 4×4×4 = 64 threads per workgroup
// Each thread processes a 16×16×16 region of voxels (4096 voxels per thread), so one workgroup covers a
// 64³ block. A segment is 128³, so it is dispatched as 2×2×2 workgroups.
layout(local_size_x = 4, local_size_y = 4, local_size_z = 4) in;

// Segment voxel texture (128³ texels, R8 format): one palette-index byte per voxel. Read with texelFetch.
layout(set = 0, binding = 0) uniform sampler3D segment_voxels;

// Output buffer: 6 uints per segment (minX, maxX, minY, maxY, minZ, maxZ)
// We use atomics to combine results from all threads
layout(set = 0, binding = 1, std430) buffer BoundsBuffer {
    uint bounds[]; // [segmentIndex * 6 + 0..5]
};

// Push constants for segment info
layout(push_constant) uniform PushConstants {
    uint segment_index;      // Which segment we're processing
    uint segment_origin_x;   // Segment origin in world coordinates (segment coord × 128)
    uint segment_origin_y;
    uint segment_origin_z;
};

// Shared memory for workgroup reduction
shared uint local_min_x, local_max_x;
shared uint local_min_y, local_max_y;
shared uint local_min_z, local_max_z;
shared bool local_has_voxels;

void main() {
    uint local_id = gl_LocalInvocationIndex;

    // Initialize shared memory (first thread only)
    if (local_id == 0) {
        local_min_x = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
        local_max_x = 0u;
        local_min_y = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
        local_max_y = 0u;
        local_min_z = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
        local_max_z = 0u;
        local_has_voxels = false;
    }
    barrier();

    // Each thread processes a 16×16×16 region of voxels, inside the 64³ block this workgroup covers
    uvec3 thread_id = gl_LocalInvocationID;
    uvec3 voxel_start = gl_WorkGroupID * 64u + thread_id * 16u;

    // Track this thread's local min/max
    uint thread_min_x = 0xFFFFFFFFu, thread_max_x = 0u;
    uint thread_min_y = 0xFFFFFFFFu, thread_max_y = 0u;
    uint thread_min_z = 0xFFFFFFFFu, thread_max_z = 0u;
    bool thread_has_voxels = false;

    for (uint vz = 0u; vz < 16u; vz++) {
        for (uint vy = 0u; vy < 16u; vy++) {
            for (uint vx = 0u; vx < 16u; vx++) {
                uvec3 voxel_coord = voxel_start + uvec3(vx, vy, vz);

                // Any non-zero palette index is a solid voxel
                if (texelFetch(segment_voxels, ivec3(voxel_coord), 0).r > 0.0) {
                    thread_has_voxels = true;

                    // Bounds are kept at brick granularity (2×2×2 voxels), so snap to the brick origin
                    // by clearing bit 0, then add the segment origin
                    uint world_x = segment_origin_x + (voxel_coord.x & ~1u);
                    uint world_y = segment_origin_y + (voxel_coord.y & ~1u);
                    uint world_z = segment_origin_z + (voxel_coord.z & ~1u);

                    thread_min_x = min(thread_min_x, world_x);
                    thread_max_x = max(thread_max_x, world_x);
                    thread_min_y = min(thread_min_y, world_y);
                    thread_max_y = max(thread_max_y, world_y);
                    thread_min_z = min(thread_min_z, world_z);
                    thread_max_z = max(thread_max_z, world_z);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Reduce to shared memory using atomics
    if (thread_has_voxels) {
        atomicMin(local_min_x, thread_min_x);
        atomicMax(local_max_x, thread_max_x);
        atomicMin(local_min_y, thread_min_y);
        atomicMax(local_max_y, thread_max_y);
        atomicMin(local_min_z, thread_min_z);
        atomicMax(local_max_z, thread_max_z);
        local_has_voxels = true;
    }

    barrier();

    // First thread writes to global buffer
    if (local_id == 0 && local_has_voxels) {
        uint base_idx = segment_index * 6u;
        atomicMin(bounds[base_idx + 0u], local_min_x);
        atomicMax(bounds[base_idx + 1u], local_max_x);
        atomicMin(bounds[base_idx + 2u], local_min_y);
        atomicMax(bounds[base_idx + 3u], local_max_y);
        atomicMin(bounds[base_idx + 4u], local_min_z);
        atomicMax(bounds[base_idx + 5u], local_max_z);
    }
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/voxel_ortho_sprite.gdshader
```
shader_type spatial;
// "unshaded": we do our own lighting. "cull_back": the proxy quad is turned to face the viewer every
// frame (see VoxelOrthoSprite.cs), so its back face is never visible.
render_mode unshaded, cull_back;

// =============================================================================
// OVERVIEW
// =============================================================================
// Volumetric ortho-sprite preview. Adapted from redot/BenVoxelGpu/volumetric_ortho_sprite.gdshader.
//
// The model is drawn like a 2D sprite: parallel rays, a chunky virtual-pixel grid and a one-pixel black
// outline, all in a single fragment pass. It is a preview only; it reads the same live voxel textures as
// the editable model (voxel_storage.gdshaderinc), so edits appear in it immediately.
//
// HOW IT WORKS:
//   1. A camera-facing QuadMesh, sized to the model's orthographic footprint plus a margin for the
//      outline, is the rasterisation footprint (VoxelOrthoSprite.cs rebuilds it every frame).
//   2. Each fragment knows where it is on that quad (quad_uv). Scaled by quad_size this is a position in
//      the sprite plane in voxel units. It is snapped to the virtual pixel grid, which has `sigma`
//      pixels per voxel.
//   3. A ray with the shared direction ray_dir_local starts at the snapped position and is marched
//      through the voxels (march_box). A hit outputs the palette colour with per-face lighting. A miss
//      tests the four cardinal neighbour pixels; if any hits, this pixel is outline.
//
// WHY THE GRID COMES FROM THE QUAD AND NOT FROM THE SCREEN (the original shader used screen position):
//   The quad is a flat card. In VR each eye sees the same card from a slightly different place, so the
//   position on the card is the same physical point for both eyes, the pixel grid is identical in both,
//   and the sprite reads as a flat billboard with no per-eye pixel shimmer. The ray direction and the
//   quad orientation come from the HEAD position (the midpoint between the eyes), not the per-eye
//   position, so both eyes also march identical rays. The quad's own depth is the sprite's depth.
//   Because nothing depends on the projection matrix, this also works unchanged on the flat screen.
//
// COORDINATE SPACE: all vectors are in Godot Y-up voxel space (one unit per voxel), the same space as
// the DDA in voxel_storage.gdshaderinc, i.e. the local space of the VoxelOrthoSprite node.
// =============================================================================

#include "res://voxel_storage.gdshaderinc"

// Direction from the viewer's head to the model centre, unit length. Shared by every ray (orthographic).
uniform vec3 ray_dir_local;

// Sprite up direction, unit length and perpendicular to ray_dir_local. It is derived from the node's own
// up, not the camera's, so the sprite stays upright relative to its own orientation.
// Sprite right is cross(ray_dir_local, sprite_up_local).
uniform vec3 sprite_up_local;

// Virtual pixels per voxel. Higher is finer; 1 makes every voxel one chunky pixel.
uniform float sigma = 4.0;

// Size of the proxy quad in voxel units. The quad is centred on the model's bounding box centre.
uniform vec2 quad_size = vec2(1.0);

// Unit vector from the model towards the light, in voxel space.
uniform vec3 light_dir = vec3(0.57735);

// Position on the unit quad, -0.5..0.5 on each axis. Interpolated perspective-correctly, so it is the
// same point on the card whichever eye is rendering.
varying vec2 quad_uv;

void vertex() {
    quad_uv = VERTEX.xy;
}

// Marches one sprite-plane ray. `offset` is a position in the sprite plane relative to the model centre,
// in voxel units. The ray starts well in front of the model and is marched through it.
uint trace_sprite_ray(vec2 offset, vec3 sprite_right, vec3 box_min, vec3 box_max, out vec3 normal) {
    // Pull the start back by twice the largest dimension so it is certainly outside the box; march_box
    // then finds the entry point itself.
    float max_dim = max(max(box_size.x, box_size.y), box_size.z);
    vec3 origin = box_center + offset.x * sprite_right + offset.y * sprite_up_local - 2.0 * max_dim * ray_dir_local;
    vec3 hit_pos;
    return march_box(origin, ray_dir_local, box_min, box_max, normal, hit_pos);
}

void fragment() {
    vec3 sprite_right = cross(ray_dir_local, sprite_up_local);
    float delta_px = 1.0 / sigma; // width of one virtual pixel in voxel units

    vec3 box_min = box_center - box_size * 0.5;
    vec3 box_max = box_center + box_size * 0.5;

    // Snap to the nearest virtual pixel centre so each virtual pixel covers a block of screen fragments.
    vec2 offset = round(quad_uv * quad_size / delta_px) * delta_px;

    vec3 normal;
    uint material = trace_sprite_ray(offset, sprite_right, box_min, box_max, normal);

    if (material > 0u) {
        // Same lighting as voxel_raymarch.gdshader: diffuse from the light plus 30% ambient. The normal
        // is one of the six axis directions, so each visible face has one flat shade.
        float lighting = max(dot(normal, light_dir), 0.0) * 0.7 + 0.3;
        ALBEDO = get_material_color(material) * lighting;
    } else {
        // Miss: outline if any of the four cardinal neighbours (no diagonals, as in classic sprite
        // outlines) hit. Anything else is empty space.
        vec3 unused;
        if (trace_sprite_ray(offset - vec2(delta_px, 0.0), sprite_right, box_min, box_max, unused) > 0u
                || trace_sprite_ray(offset + vec2(delta_px, 0.0), sprite_right, box_min, box_max, unused) > 0u
                || trace_sprite_ray(offset + vec2(0.0, delta_px), sprite_right, box_min, box_max, unused) > 0u
                || trace_sprite_ray(offset - vec2(0.0, delta_px), sprite_right, box_min, box_max, unused) > 0u) {
            ALBEDO = vec3(0.0);
        } else {
            discard;
        }
    }
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/voxel_raymarch.gdshader
```
shader_type spatial;
// cull_front: only back faces rasterise, so the box footprint is covered whether the eye is outside or inside it.
// The ray for each pixel is rebuilt from the eye through SCREEN_UV (not from the interpolated vertex position),
// so the six box faces never produce seams. DEPTH is written at the hit voxel, so no depth_draw_* mode is needed.
render_mode unshaded, cull_front;

// =============================================================================
// OVERVIEW
// =============================================================================
// Bounding-box mesh + DDA raymarcher for sparse, segmented voxel models.
//
// HOW IT WORKS:
//   1. A BoxMesh sized to the model's bounds (see Root.cs) is the rasterisation footprint. Its only job
//      is to make the GPU run the fragment shader for every pixel the model could cover.
//   2. The fragment shader ignores the box geometry. For each pixel it casts a ray from the eye
//      through that pixel, intersects it analytically with the bounding box (ray-AABB slab test) and
//      walks the voxel grid from the entry point with a DDA (Digital Differential Analyser) until it
//      finds a non-empty voxel or leaves the box.
//   3. On a hit it outputs the palette colour (with simple per-face lighting) and writes DEPTH at the
//      hit point. On a miss the pixel is discarded.
//
// WHY THE RAY IS NOT TAKEN FROM THE BOX SURFACE:
//   An earlier version started the ray at the interpolated box-surface position. A voxel face lying
//   exactly on the box surface (the model's top face always does) then sat on a floor() boundary, and
//   per-pixel float error decided which voxel the DDA started in. That produced fuzzy faces, wrong
//   first-hit lighting, and (for faces at coordinate 0) vanishing faces. Intersecting the ray with the
//   box analytically and clamping the origin just inside it removes the whole class of problem.
//
// COORDINATE SPACES:
//   World space       - Godot Y-up right-handed.
//   Mesh-local space  - world transformed by inv_model_matrix; origin = box centre.
//   Voxel space       - mesh-local + box_center. Godot Y-up, one unit per voxel, box corner at
//                       box_center - box_size / 2. The DDA runs here.
//   Stored voxel data is Z-up (MagicaVoxel convention), so fetch_voxel() swaps Y and Z:
//                       (x, y, z) Y-up voxel space  ->  (x, z, y) Z-up storage.
//
// VOXEL STORAGE (see VoxelBridge.cs and voxel_storage.gdshaderinc, which holds the fetch and DDA code
// shared with voxel_ortho_sprite.gdshader):
//   The model is split into 128^3-voxel segments. Each segment is a 128^3 R8 3D texture, one texel
//   per voxel, holding the voxel's palette index. A small directory
//   texture lists which segments exist and which texture-array slot each one lives in.
//
// VR (OpenXR multiview):
//   VIEW_MATRIX / INV_VIEW_MATRIX / PROJECTION_MATRIX are the MIDPOINT (cyclopean) matrices and are the
//   same for both eyes. EYE_OFFSET is the per-eye position in that midpoint view space (+-IPD/2 on X;
//   zero when not rendering in VR). The per-eye ray origin and direction are derived from it in
//   fragment(), which is what produces correct stereo parallax. The shader never reads the vertex
//   position, so there is no vertex() function and nothing to go wrong across multiview.

#include "res://voxel_storage.gdshaderinc"

// =============================================================================
// UNIFORMS
// =============================================================================
// The voxel storage uniforms (segments, voxels, palette, box_center, box_size)
// are declared in voxel_storage.gdshaderinc.

// Inverse of the MeshInstance's GlobalTransform, precomputed on the CPU every frame (Root._Process).
// Godot provides MODEL_MATRIX in the fragment stage but not its inverse, and inverting a matrix per
// fragment would be wasteful. Because this includes the full node transform, the box can be moved,
// rotated or scaled (for example under a VR controller) and the rays follow automatically.
uniform mat4 inv_model_matrix;

// Position of the point light in voxel space (Root.cs converts the light sphere's world position every
// frame). Only the position matters; a point light has no orientation.
uniform vec3 light_position = vec3(50.0, 50.0, 25.0);

// =============================================================================
// FRAGMENT SHADER
// =============================================================================

void fragment() {
    // ---- Per-eye ray ----
    // In VR multiview, INV_VIEW_MATRIX is the midpoint (cyclopean) view and EYE_OFFSET is the
    // per-eye offset in that view space (zero when not in VR). Treating EYE_OFFSET as a point in
    // midpoint view space and transforming it to world space gives this eye's position; using
    // vec4(0,0,0,1) instead would give the midpoint, identical for both eyes, and no stereo parallax.
    vec3 camera_world = (INV_VIEW_MATRIX * vec4(EYE_OFFSET, 1.0)).xyz;

    // Unproject this pixel through the (midpoint) projection into view space, then aim the ray from
    // the per-eye position through that point. Using EYE_OFFSET for the direction as well as the
    // origin keeps the two consistent. w = 0 in INV_VIEW_MATRIX applies rotation only.
    vec4 view_frag = INV_PROJECTION_MATRIX * vec4(SCREEN_UV * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    view_frag /= view_frag.w;
    vec3 ray_dir_view = normalize(view_frag.xyz - EYE_OFFSET);
    vec3 ray_world_dir = normalize((INV_VIEW_MATRIX * vec4(ray_dir_view, 0.0)).xyz);

    // ---- World space -> voxel space ----
    // The point transform gives mesh-local coordinates (origin at the box centre); adding box_center
    // gives voxel coordinates. The direction uses w = 0 (no translation) and is renormalised because
    // the inverse of a scaled matrix changes vector length.
    vec3 camera_voxel = (inv_model_matrix * vec4(camera_world, 1.0)).xyz + box_center;
    vec3 ray_dir = normalize((inv_model_matrix * vec4(ray_world_dir, 0.0)).xyz);
    vec3 box_min = box_center - box_size * 0.5;
    vec3 box_max = box_center + box_size * 0.5;

    // ---- March ----
    // See march_box() in voxel_storage.gdshaderinc for the box intersection and the DDA.
    vec3 normal;
    vec3 hit_pos;
    uint material = march_box(camera_voxel, ray_dir, box_min, box_max, normal, hit_pos);
    if (material == 0u) discard; // Miss: leave the pixel untouched (and keep the material opaque)

    // Point light at light_position (voxel space) plus 30% ambient, with no distance falloff. Faces
    // turned away from the light are not darker than ambient. Wrapped in max() so back-facing
    // normals clamp to 0.
    vec3 to_light = normalize(light_position - hit_pos);
    float lighting = max(dot(normal, to_light), 0.0) * 0.7 + 0.3;
    ALBEDO = get_material_color(material) * lighting;

    // ---- Depth at the hit voxel surface ----
    // Without this the depth buffer would hold the depth of the box's back face. That would make other
    // geometry intersect the model as if it were a solid box, and in VR the OpenXR compositor uses
    // depth for reprojection (ATW/ASW), so a flat box would be warped as a plane and cancel the per-eye
    // parallax the DDA computed.
    // Chain: voxel space -> mesh-local (subtract box_center) -> world (MODEL_MATRIX) -> clip space.
    vec4 hit_clip = PROJECTION_MATRIX * VIEW_MATRIX * MODEL_MATRIX * vec4(hit_pos - box_center, 1.0);
    DEPTH = hit_clip.z / hit_clip.w;
}
```

### redot/BenVoxelEditor/BoundsCompute.cs
```
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using BenVoxel.Models;
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxelEditor;

/// <summary>
/// Manages GPU compute shader for bounds calculation.
/// Processes segment textures to find min/max voxel coordinates.
///
/// Synchronous execution with throttling - GPU parallelism makes this fast.
/// Godot 4 doesn't support true async GPU readback, so we embrace the sync
/// but benefit from GPU parallelism for the actual computation.
/// </summary>
public sealed class BoundsCompute : IDisposable
{
	// Bounds result: 6 uints (minX, maxX, minY, maxY, minZ, maxZ) per segment
	private const int BoundsPerSegment = 6;
	private const int MaxSegments = 256;

	private readonly RenderingDevice _rd;
	private Rid _shader;
	private Rid _pipeline;
	private Rid _boundsBuffer;

	// Throttling to avoid computing every frame
	private double _lastTrimTime;
	private const double ThrottleIntervalSeconds = 0.1; // Max once per 100ms

	/// <summary>
	/// Whether the compute shader is available and initialized.
	/// </summary>
	public bool IsAvailable => _shader.IsValid && _pipeline.IsValid && _boundsBuffer.IsValid;

	public BoundsCompute()
	{
		// Use the main RenderingDevice to access textures from the rendering pipeline
		// via RenderingServer.TextureGetRdTexture()
		_rd = RenderingServer.GetRenderingDevice();
		if (_rd == null)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: RenderingDevice not available");
			return;
		}

		InitializeShader();
		InitializeBuffers();
	}

	private void InitializeShader()
	{
		// Load and compile the compute shader
		RDShaderFile shaderFile = GD.Load<RDShaderFile>("res://bounds_reduction.glsl");
		if (shaderFile == null)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: Failed to load bounds_reduction.glsl");
			return;
		}

		RDShaderSpirV spirv = shaderFile.GetSpirV();
		if (spirv == null)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: Failed to get SPIR-V from shader");
			return;
		}

		_shader = _rd.ShaderCreateFromSpirV(spirv);
		if (!_shader.IsValid)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: Failed to create shader");
			return;
		}

		_pipeline = _rd.ComputePipelineCreate(_shader);
		if (!_pipeline.IsValid)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: Failed to create compute pipeline");
			return;
		}

		GD.Print("BoundsCompute: Shader initialized successfully");
	}

	private void InitializeBuffers()
	{
		if (!_shader.IsValid) return;

		// Create output buffer for bounds results
		// 6 uints per segment × max segments × 4 bytes per uint
		int bufferSize = BoundsPerSegment * MaxSegments * sizeof(uint);
		byte[] initialData = new byte[bufferSize];

		_boundsBuffer = _rd.StorageBufferCreate((uint)bufferSize, initialData);
		if (!_boundsBuffer.IsValid)
		{
			GD.PrintErr("BoundsCompute: Failed to create bounds buffer");
			return;
		}
```
