### src/BenVoxel.FileToVoxCore/VoxFileModel.cs
```
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using BenVoxel.Interfaces;
using BenVoxel.Models;
using FileToVoxCore.Schematics;
using FileToVoxCore.Vox;
using FileToVoxCoreColor = FileToVoxCore.Drawing.Color;

namespace BenVoxel.FileToVoxCore;

public class VoxFileModel : DictionaryModel
{
	#region Read
	public VoxFileModel(VoxModel model, int frame = 0, bool incluePalette = true)
	{
		if (incluePalette)
		{
			Palette = new uint[256];
			uint[] palette = [.. model.Palette.Take(Palette.Length).Select(Color)];
			Array.Copy(
				sourceArray: palette,
				sourceIndex: 0,
				destinationArray: Palette,
				destinationIndex: 1,
				length: Math.Min(palette.Length, Palette.Length) - 1);
		}
		VoxelData voxelData = model.VoxelFrames[frame];
		SizeX = (ushort)(voxelData.VoxelsWide - 1);
		SizeY = (ushort)(voxelData.VoxelsTall - 1);
		SizeZ = (ushort)(voxelData.VoxelsDeep - 1);
		foreach (KeyValuePair<int, byte> voxel in voxelData.Colors)
		{
			voxelData.Get3DPos(voxel.Key, out int x, out int y, out int z);
			this[(ushort)x, (ushort)y, (ushort)z] = voxel.Value;
		}
	}
	public VoxFileModel(string filePath, int frame = 0) : this(new VoxReader().LoadModel(filePath), frame) { }
	public VoxFileModel(Stream stream, int frame = 0) : this(new VoxReader().LoadModel(stream), frame) { }
	public static VoxFileModel[] Models(Stream stream)
	{
		VoxModel model = new VoxReader().LoadModel(stream);
		return [.. Enumerable.Range(0, model.VoxelFrames.Count()).Select(i => new VoxFileModel(model, i))];
	}
	public static VoxFileModel[] Models(Stream stream, out uint[] palette)
	{
		VoxModel model = new VoxReader().LoadModel(stream);
		palette = new uint[256];
		uint[] sourceArray = [.. model.Palette.Take(palette.Length).Select(Color)];
		Array.Copy(
			sourceArray: sourceArray,
			sourceIndex: 0,
			destinationArray: palette,
			destinationIndex: 1,
			length: Math.Min(palette.Length, sourceArray.Length) - 1);
		return [.. Enumerable.Range(0, model.VoxelFrames.Count()).Select(i => new VoxFileModel(model, i, false))];
	}
	public uint[] Palette { get; set; }
	public static uint Color(FileToVoxCoreColor color) => Argb2rgba((uint)color.ToArgb());
	/// <param name="rgba">argb8888, Big Endian</param>
	/// <returns>rgba8888, Big Endian</returns>
	public static uint Argb2rgba(uint argb) => argb << 8 | argb >> 24;
	#endregion Read
	#region Write
	public static FileToVoxCoreColor Color(uint color) => FileToVoxCoreColor.FromArgb(
		alpha: (byte)color,
		red: (byte)(color >> 24),
		green: (byte)(color >> 16),
		blue: (byte)(color >> 8));
	public static IEnumerable<Voxel> FileToVoxCoreVoxels(IModel model, uint[] palette) => model
		.Select(voxel => new Voxel(
			x: voxel.X,
			y: voxel.Y,
			z: voxel.Z,
			color: Rgba2argb(palette[voxel.Material])));
	public static bool Write(string absolutePath, uint[] palette, IModel model) =>
		new VoxWriter().WriteModel(
			absolutePath: absolutePath,
			palette: [.. palette.Skip(1).Select(Color)],
			schematic: new Schematic(FileToVoxCoreVoxels(model, palette).ToList()));
	/// <param name="rgba">rgba8888, Big Endian</param>
	/// <returns>argb8888, Big Endian</returns>
	public static uint Rgba2argb(uint rgba) => rgba << 24 | rgba >> 8;
	#endregion Write
}
```

### src/BenVoxel/BenVoxelFile.cs
```
using System;
using System.Buffers.Binary;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
using BenVoxel.Interfaces;
using BenVoxel.Models;
using BenVoxel.Structs;

namespace BenVoxel;

public class BenVoxelFile() : IBinaryWritable
{
	[JsonPropertyName("version")]
	public string Version { get; set; } = "0.1";
	#region Nested classes
	public class Metadata() : IBinaryWritable
	{
		#region Data
		[JsonIgnore]
		public SanitizedKeyDictionary<string> Properties { get; set; } = [];
		[JsonPropertyName("properties")]
		[JsonIgnore(Condition = JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull)]
		public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> PropertiesReadOnly
		{
			get => Properties.Any() ? new ReadOnlyDictionary<string, string>(Properties) : null;
			set
			{
				Properties.Clear();
				if (value is null) return;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> pair in value)
					Properties.Add(pair);
			}
		}
		[JsonIgnore]
		public SanitizedKeyDictionary<Point3D> Points { get; set; } = [];
		[JsonPropertyName("points")]
		[JsonIgnore(Condition = JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull)]
		public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, Point3D> PointsReadOnly
		{
			get => Points.Any() ? new ReadOnlyDictionary<string, Point3D>(Points) : null;
			set
			{
				Points.Clear();
				if (value is null) return;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, Point3D> pair in value)
					Points.Add(pair);
			}
		}
		[JsonIgnore]
		public SanitizedKeyDictionary<Color[]> Palettes { get; set; } = [];
		[JsonPropertyName("palettes")]
		[JsonIgnore(Condition = JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull)]
		public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, Color[]> PalettesReadOnly
		{
			get => Palettes.Any() ? new ReadOnlyDictionary<string, Color[]>(Palettes) : null;
			set
			{
				Palettes.Clear();
				if (value is null) return;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, Color[]> pair in value)
					Palettes.Add(pair);
			}
		}
		[JsonIgnore]
		public SanitizedKeyDictionary<JsonElement> Json { get; set; } = [];
		[JsonPropertyName("json")]
		[JsonIgnore(Condition = JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull)]
		public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, JsonElement> JsonReadOnly
		{
			get => Json.Any() ? new ReadOnlyDictionary<string, JsonElement>(Json) : null;
			set
			{
				Json.Clear();
				if (value is null) return;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, JsonElement> pair in value)
					Json.Add(pair);
			}
		}
		#endregion Data
		#region Metadata
		/// <summary>
		/// Gets and sets palettes without descriptions
		/// </summary>
		public uint[] this[string paletteName]
		{
			get => [.. Palettes[paletteName].Take(256).Select(color => color.Rgba)];
			set => Palettes[paletteName] = [.. Color.Colors(value.Take(256))];
		}
		public Metadata(Stream stream) : this()
		{
			using BinaryReader reader = new(
				input: stream,
				encoding: Encoding.UTF8,
				leaveOpen: true);
			FromReader(reader);
		}
		public Metadata(BinaryReader reader) : this() => FromReader(reader);
		private void FromReader(BinaryReader reader)
		{
			while (reader.BaseStream.Position < reader.BaseStream.Length - 4
				&& reader.TryRIFF((reader, fourCC) =>
				{
					switch (fourCC)
					{
						case "PROP":
							ushort count = reader.ReadUInt16();
							for (ushort i = 0; i < count; i++)
								Properties[ReadKey(reader)] = ReadString(reader);
							return true;
						case "PT3D":
							count = reader.ReadUInt16();
							for (ushort i = 0; i < count; i++)
								Points[ReadKey(reader)] = new Point3D(reader);
							return true;
						case "PALC":
							count = reader.ReadUInt16();
							for (ushort i = 0; i < count; i++)
							{
								string key = ReadKey(reader);
								uint[] colors = [.. Enumerable.Range(0, reader.ReadByte() + 1).Select(i => BinaryPrimitives.ReverseEndianness(reader.ReadUInt32()))];
								bool hasDescriptions = reader.ReadByte() != 0;
								Palettes[key] = [.. colors.Select(rgba => new Color
								{
									Rgba = rgba,
									Description = hasDescriptions ? ReadString(reader) : null,
								})];
							}
							return true;
						case "JSON":
							count = reader.ReadUInt16();
							for (ushort i = 0; i < count; i++)
							{
								string key = ReadKey(reader);
								Json[key] = JsonDocument.Parse(ReadString(reader)).RootElement.Clone();
							}
							return true;
						default:
							return false;
					}
				})) { }
		}
		public bool Any() => Properties.Any() || Points.Any() || Palettes.Any() || Json.Any();
		#endregion Metadata
		#region IBinaryWritable
		public void Write(Stream stream)
		{
			using BinaryWriter writer = new(
				output: stream,
				encoding: Encoding.UTF8,
				leaveOpen: true);
			Write(writer);
		}
		public void Write(BinaryWriter writer)
		{
			if (Properties.Any())
				writer.RIFF("PROP", writer =>
				{
					writer.Write((ushort)Properties.Count());
					foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> property in Properties)
					{
						WriteKey(writer, property.Key);
						WriteString(writer, property.Value);
					}
				});
			if (Points.Any())
				writer.RIFF("PT3D", writer =>
				{
					writer.Write((ushort)Points.Count());
					foreach (KeyValuePair<string, Point3D> point in Points)
					{
						WriteKey(writer, point.Key);
						point.Value.Write(writer);
					}
				});
			if (Palettes.Any())
				writer.RIFF("PALC", writer =>
				{
					writer.Write((ushort)Palettes.Count());
					foreach (KeyValuePair<string, Color[]> palette in Palettes)
					{
						WriteKey(writer, palette.Key);
						writer.Write((byte)(palette.Value.Length - 1));
						foreach (Color color in palette.Value)
							writer.Write(BinaryPrimitives.ReverseEndianness(color.Rgba));
						if (palette.Value.Any(color => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(color.Description)))
						{
							writer.Write((byte)1);
							foreach (Color color in palette.Value)
								WriteString(writer, color.Description ?? "");
						}
						else
							writer.Write((byte)0);
					}
				});
			if (Json.Any())
				writer.RIFF("JSON", writer =>
				{
					writer.Write((ushort)Json.Count());
					foreach (KeyValuePair<string, JsonElement> json in Json)
					{
						WriteKey(writer, json.Key);
						WriteString(writer, JsonSerializer.Serialize(json.Value));
					}
				});
		}
		#endregion IBinaryWritable
	}
	public readonly record struct Color()
	{
		#region Data
		[JsonIgnore]
		public uint Rgba { get; init; } = 0u;
		[JsonPropertyName("rgba")]
		public string RgbaHex
		{
			get => $"#{Rgba:X8}";
			init => Rgba = uint.Parse(value[1..], System.Globalization.NumberStyles.HexNumber);
		}
		[JsonPropertyName("description")]
		[JsonIgnore(Condition = JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull)]
		public string Description { get; init; } = null;
		#endregion Data
		public static IEnumerable<Color> Colors(IEnumerable<uint> colors) => colors?.Select(rgba => new Color { Rgba = rgba, });
		public static IEnumerable<uint> Colors(IEnumerable<Color> colors) => colors?.Select(color => color.Rgba);
	}
	public class Model() : IBinaryWritable
	{
		#region Data
		[JsonPropertyName("metadata")]
		[JsonIgnore(Condition = JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull)]
		public Metadata Metadata { get; set; } = null;
		[JsonIgnore]
		public DictionaryModel Geometry { get; set; } = null;
		[JsonPropertyName("geometry")]
		public SvoModel SvoModel
		{
			get => Geometry is null ? new() : new(Geometry);
			set => Geometry = new DictionaryModel(value);
		}
		#endregion Data
		#region Model
		public Model(Stream stream) : this()
		{
			using BinaryReader reader = new(
				input: stream,
				encoding: Encoding.UTF8,
				leaveOpen: true);
			FromReader(reader);
		}
		public Model(BinaryReader reader) : this() => FromReader(reader);
		private void FromReader(BinaryReader reader)
		{
			reader.TryRIFF((reader, fourCC) =>
				{
					if (!"DATA".Equals(fourCC))
						return false;
					Metadata = new(reader);
					return true;
				});
			if (!reader.TryRIFF((reader, fourCC) =>
				{
					if (!"SVOG".Equals(fourCC))
						return false;
					SvoModel = new(reader);
					return true;
				}))
				throw new IOException("Expected \"SVOG\"");
		}
		#endregion Model
		#region IBinaryWritable
		public void Write(Stream stream)
		{
			using BinaryWriter writer = new(
				output: stream,
				encoding: Encoding.UTF8,
				leaveOpen: true);
			Write(writer);
		}
		public void Write(BinaryWriter writer)
		{
			Metadata?.RIFF("DATA", writer);
			SvoModel.RIFF("SVOG", writer);
		}
		#endregion IBinaryWritable
	}
	#endregion Nested classes
	#region Data
	[JsonPropertyName("metadata")]
	[JsonIgnore(Condition = JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull)]
	public Metadata Global { get; set; } = null;
	[JsonPropertyName("models")]
	public SanitizedKeyDictionary<Model> Models { get; set; } = [];
	#endregion Data
	#region BenVoxelFile
	public BenVoxelFile(Stream stream) : this()
	{
		using BinaryReader reader = new(
			input: stream,
			encoding: Encoding.UTF8,
			leaveOpen: true);
		FromReader(reader);
	}
	public BenVoxelFile(BinaryReader reader) : this() => FromReader(reader);
	private void FromReader(BinaryReader reader)
	{
		if (!FourCC(reader).Equals("BENV"))
			throw new IOException("Expected \"BENV\"");
		uint totalLength = reader.ReadUInt32();
		string version = ReadKey(reader);
		using MemoryStream decompressedStream = new();
		using (MemoryStream compressedStream = new(reader.ReadBytes((int)(totalLength - version.Length - 1))))
		using (DeflateStream deflateStream = new(
			stream: compressedStream,
			mode: CompressionMode.Decompress))
		{
			deflateStream.CopyTo(decompressedStream);
		}
		decompressedStream.Position = 0;
		using BinaryReader decompressedReader = new(
			input: decompressedStream,
			encoding: Encoding.UTF8);
		decompressedReader.TryRIFF((reader, fourCC) =>
		{
			if (!"DATA".Equals(fourCC))
				return false;
			Global = new(reader);
			return true;
		});
		ushort count = decompressedReader.ReadUInt16();
		for (ushort i = 0; i < count; i++)
		{
			string name = ReadKey(decompressedReader);
			if (!decompressedReader.TryRIFF((reader, fourCC) =>
			{
				if (!"MODL".Equals(fourCC))
					return false;
				Models[name] = new(reader);
				return true;
			}))
				throw new IOException("Expected \"MODL\"");
		}
	}
	public static BenVoxelFile Load(string path)
	{
		using FileStream fileStream = new(
			path: path,
			mode: FileMode.Open,
			access: FileAccess.Read);
		return (".json".Equals(Path.GetExtension(path), StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) ?
			JsonSerializer.Deserialize<BenVoxelFile>(fileStream)
			: new(fileStream))
			?? throw new NullReferenceException($"Loading failed: \"{path}\"");
	}
	public static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonSerializerOptions = new()
	{
		IndentCharacter = '\t',
		IndentSize = 1,
		WriteIndented = true,
	};
	public BenVoxelFile Save(string path, JsonSerializerOptions jsonSerializerOptions = null)
	{
		if (".json".Equals(Path.GetExtension(path), StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			File.WriteAllText(path: path, contents: JsonSerializer.Serialize(this, jsonSerializerOptions ?? JsonSerializerOptions));
		else
			using (FileStream fileStream = new(
				path: path,
				mode: FileMode.OpenOrCreate,
				access: FileAccess.Write))
			{
				Write(fileStream);
			}
		return this;
	}
	public Color[] GetColors(string modelName = "", string paletteName = "") =>
		Models.TryGetValue(modelName, out Model model)
			&& (model.Metadata?.PalettesReadOnly?.TryGetValue(paletteName, out Color[] palette) ?? false) ? palette
			: Global?.PalettesReadOnly?.TryGetValue(paletteName, out palette) ?? false ? palette
			: null;
	public uint[] GetPalette(string modelName = "", string paletteName = "") => Color.Colors(GetColors(modelName, paletteName))?.ToArray();
	public string GetProperty(string modelName = "", string propertyName = "") =>
		Models.TryGetValue(modelName, out Model model)
			&& (model.Metadata?.PropertiesReadOnly?.TryGetValue(propertyName, out string property) ?? false) ? property
			: Global?.PropertiesReadOnly?.TryGetValue(propertyName, out property) ?? false ? property
			: null;
	public Point3D? GetPoint(string modelName = "", string pointName = "") =>
		Models.TryGetValue(modelName, out Model model)
			&& (model.Metadata?.PointsReadOnly?.TryGetValue(pointName, out Point3D point) ?? false) ? point
			: Global?.PointsReadOnly?.TryGetValue(pointName, out point) ?? false ? point
			: null;
	public JsonElement? GetJson(string modelName = "", string jsonName = "") =>
		Models.TryGetValue(modelName, out Model model)
			&& (model.Metadata?.JsonReadOnly?.TryGetValue(jsonName, out JsonElement json) ?? false) ? json
			: Global?.JsonReadOnly?.TryGetValue(jsonName, out json) ?? false ? json
			: null;
	public Model DefaultModel()
	{
		if (!Models.TryGetValue("", out Model benVoxelFileModel))
			benVoxelFileModel = Models.FirstOrDefault().Value;
		return benVoxelFileModel;
	}
	public DictionaryModel Default(out uint[] palette)
	{
		Model benVoxelFileModel = DefaultModel();
		DictionaryModel geometry = benVoxelFileModel?.Geometry;
		if (!(benVoxelFileModel?.Metadata?.Palettes.TryGetValue("", out Color[] colors) ?? false)
			&& !(Global?.Palettes.TryGetValue("", out colors) ?? false))
			colors = benVoxelFileModel?.Metadata?.Palettes?.Any() ?? false ?
				benVoxelFileModel.Metadata.Palettes.First().Value
				: Global?.Palettes?.Any() ?? false ?
					Global.Palettes.First().Value
					: null;
		palette = colors is null ? null : [.. colors.Take(256).Select(color => color.Rgba)];
		return geometry;
	}
	#endregion BenVoxelFile
	#region IBinaryWritable
	public void Write(Stream stream)
	{
		using BinaryWriter writer = new(
			output: stream,
			encoding: Encoding.UTF8,
			leaveOpen: true);
		Write(writer);
	}
	public void Write(BinaryWriter writer)
	{
		using MemoryStream compressedStream = new();
		using (DeflateStream deflateStream = new(
			stream: compressedStream,
			mode: CompressionMode.Compress,
			leaveOpen: true))
		using (BinaryWriter deflateWriter = new(
			output: deflateStream,
			encoding: Encoding.UTF8,
			leaveOpen: true))
		{
			Global?.RIFF("DATA", deflateWriter);
			deflateWriter.Write((ushort)Models.Count);
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, Model> model in Models)
			{
				WriteKey(deflateWriter, model.Key);
				model.Value.RIFF("MODL", deflateWriter);
			}
			deflateWriter.Flush();
		}
		writer.Write(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("BENV"));
		writer.Write((uint)(Version.Length + 1 + compressedStream.Length));
		WriteKey(writer, Version);
		writer.Write(compressedStream.ToArray());
	}
	#endregion IBinaryWritable
	#region Utilities
	public static string FourCC(BinaryReader reader) => Encoding.UTF8.GetString(reader.ReadBytes(4));
	public static string ReadKey(BinaryReader reader) => Encoding.UTF8.GetString(reader.ReadBytes(reader.ReadByte()));
	public static void WriteKey(BinaryWriter writer, string s)
	{
		writer.Write((byte)s.Length);
		writer.Write(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(s));
	}
	public static string ReadString(BinaryReader reader) => Encoding.UTF8.GetString(reader.ReadBytes((int)reader.ReadUInt32()));
	public static void WriteString(BinaryWriter writer, string s)
	{
		byte[] bytes = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(s);
		writer.Write((uint)bytes.Length);
		writer.Write(bytes);
	}
	#endregion Utilities
}
```

### src/BenVoxel/ExtensionMethods.cs
```
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using BenVoxel.Interfaces;
using BenVoxel.Structs;

namespace BenVoxel;

public static class ExtensionMethods
{
	#region IModel
	/// <summary>
	/// Checking for being out of bounds can involve fewer comparisons than checking for being in bounds.
	/// </summary>
	/// <param name="coordinates">3D coordinates</param>
	/// <returns>true if coordinates are outside the bounds of the model</returns>
	public static bool IsOutside(this IModel model, params ushort[] coordinates) => coordinates[0] >= model.SizeX || coordinates[1] >= model.SizeY || coordinates[2] >= model.SizeZ;
	public static Point3D Center(this IModel model) => new(model.SizeX >> 1, model.SizeY >> 1, model.SizeZ >> 1);
	public static Point3D BottomCenter(this IModel model) => new(model.SizeX >> 1, model.SizeY >> 1, 0);
	public static byte Set(this IEditableModel model, Voxel voxel) => model[voxel.X, voxel.Y, voxel.Z] = voxel.Material;
	#endregion IModel
	#region IBinaryWritable
	/// <summary>
	/// Writes a RIFF chunk with content provided by a delegate
	/// </summary>
	/// <param name="writer">The BinaryWriter to write to</param>
	/// <param name="fourCC">4-character ASCII identifier for the chunk</param>
	/// <param name="writeContent">Action that writes the chunk content</param>
	/// <returns>The writer for method chaining</returns>
	public static BinaryWriter RIFF(this BinaryWriter writer, string fourCC, Action<BinaryWriter> writeContent)
	{
		if (fourCC.Length != 4)
			throw new ArgumentException(message: "RIFF chunk identifier must be exactly 4 characters", paramName: nameof(fourCC));
		using MemoryStream contentStream = new();
		using (BinaryWriter contentWriter = new(
			output: contentStream,
			encoding: Encoding.UTF8,
			leaveOpen: true))
			writeContent(contentWriter);
		byte[] content = contentStream.ToArray();
		writer.Write(Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes(fourCC), 0, 4);
		writer.Write((uint)content.Length);
		writer.Write(content);
		writer.Flush();
		return writer;
	}
	/// <summary>
	/// Writes an IBinaryWritable object as a RIFF chunk
	/// </summary>
	/// <param name="writer">The BinaryWriter to write to</param>
	/// <param name="fourCC">4-character ASCII identifier for the chunk</param> 
	/// <param name="writable">The object to write as chunk content</param>
	/// <returns>The writer for method chaining</returns>
	public static BinaryWriter RIFF(this IBinaryWritable writable, string fourCC, BinaryWriter writer) => writer.RIFF(fourCC, writable.Write);
	/// <summary>
	/// Reads a RIFF chunk header and provides a reader limited to the chunk's content
	/// </summary>
	/// <param name="reader">The source reader</param>
	/// <param name="readContent">Delegate that receives a length-limited reader for the chunk content</param>
	/// <returns>The FourCC identifier of the chunk that was read</returns>
	public static string RIFF(this BinaryReader reader, Action<BinaryReader, string> readContent)
	{
		string fourCC = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(reader.ReadBytes(4));
		uint length = reader.ReadUInt32();
		using MemoryStream limitedStream = new(reader.ReadBytes((int)length));
		using BinaryReader limitedReader = new(
			input: limitedStream,
			encoding: Encoding.UTF8,
			leaveOpen: true);
		readContent(limitedReader, fourCC);
		return fourCC;
	}
	public static bool TryRIFF(this BinaryReader reader, Func<BinaryReader, string, bool> readContent)
	{
		long startPosition = reader.BaseStream.Position;
		string fourCC = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(reader.ReadBytes(4));
		uint length = reader.ReadUInt32();
		using MemoryStream limitedStream = new(reader.ReadBytes((int)length));
		using BinaryReader limitedReader = new(
			input: limitedStream,
			encoding: Encoding.UTF8,
			leaveOpen: true);
		if (readContent(limitedReader, fourCC))
			return true;
		reader.BaseStream.Position = startPosition;
		return false;
	}
	#endregion IBinaryWritable
}
```

### src/BenVoxel/SanitizedKeyDictionary.cs
```
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;

namespace BenVoxel;

/// <summary>
/// Restricts keys to max length 255 with last-in-wins behavior.
/// </summary>
public sealed class SanitizedKeyDictionary<T>() : IDictionary<string, T>
{
	private readonly Dictionary<string, T> _dictionary = [];
	public static string SanitizeKey(string key)
	{
		key = key.Trim();
		return key[..Math.Min(key.Length, 255)];
	}
	#region IDictionary
	public T this[string key]
	{
		get => _dictionary[SanitizeKey(key)];
		set => _dictionary[SanitizeKey(key)] = value;
	}
	public ICollection<string> Keys => _dictionary.Keys;
	public ICollection<T> Values => _dictionary.Values;
	public int Count => _dictionary.Count;
	public bool IsReadOnly => ((ICollection<KeyValuePair<string, T>>)_dictionary).IsReadOnly;
	public void Add(string key, T value) => this[key] = value;
	public void Add(KeyValuePair<string, T> item) => this[item.Key] = item.Value;
	public void Clear() => _dictionary.Clear();
	public bool Contains(KeyValuePair<string, T> item) =>
		_dictionary.TryGetValue(SanitizeKey(item.Key), out T value)
			&& EqualityComparer<T>.Default.Equals(value, item.Value);
	public bool ContainsKey(string key) => _dictionary.ContainsKey(SanitizeKey(key));
	public void CopyTo(KeyValuePair<string, T>[] array, int arrayIndex)
	{
		if (array == null)
			throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(array));
		if (arrayIndex < 0)
			throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(arrayIndex));
		if (array.Length - arrayIndex < Count)
			throw new ArgumentException("Destination array is not large enough.");
		foreach (KeyValuePair<string, T> pair in _dictionary)
			array[arrayIndex++] = new KeyValuePair<string, T>(pair.Key, pair.Value);
	}
	public IEnumerator<KeyValuePair<string, T>> GetEnumerator() => _dictionary.GetEnumerator();
	public bool Remove(string key) => _dictionary.Remove(SanitizeKey(key));
	public bool Remove(KeyValuePair<string, T> item)
	{
		string sanitizedKey = SanitizeKey(item.Key);
		if (_dictionary.TryGetValue(sanitizedKey, out T value) &&
			EqualityComparer<T>.Default.Equals(value, item.Value))
			return _dictionary.Remove(sanitizedKey);
		return false;
	}
	public bool TryGetValue(string key, out T value) => _dictionary.TryGetValue(SanitizeKey(key), out value);
	IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator() => ((IEnumerable)_dictionary).GetEnumerator();
	#endregion IDictionary
	#region JSON
	[JsonInclude]
	public ReadOnlyDictionary<string, T> ReadOnlyDictionary
	{
		get => new(this);
		set
		{
			_dictionary.Clear();
			if (value is null) return;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, T> pair in value)
				Add(pair.Key, pair.Value);
		}
	}
	#endregion JSON
}
```

### src/Voxel2Pixel.ImageSharp/ImageMaker.cs
```
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using SixLabors.ImageSharp;
using Voxel2Pixel.Interfaces;
using Voxel2Pixel.Render;
using static Voxel2Pixel.Draw.PixelDraw;

namespace Voxel2Pixel.ImageSharp;

/// <summary>
/// Glues Voxel2Pixel to SixLabors.ImageSharp.
/// This feature is kept out of the Voxel2Pixel library so that it can stay smaller, on .NET Standard 2.0 and MIT-licensed.
/// </summary>
public static class ImageMaker
{
	public const int DefaultFrameDelay = 100;
	#region ImageSharp
	public static Image<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32> Png(ushort width = 0, params byte[] bytes)
	{
		if (width < 1)
			width = (ushort)Math.Sqrt(bytes.Length >> 2);
		return SixLabors.ImageSharp.Image.LoadPixelData<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32>(
			data: bytes,
			width: width,
			height: (bytes.Length >> 2) / width);
	}
	public static Image<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32> Png(this ISprite sprite) => SixLabors.ImageSharp.Image.LoadPixelData<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32>(
		data: sprite.Texture,
		width: sprite.Width,
		height: sprite.Height);
	public static Image<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32> AnimatedGif(int frameDelay = DefaultFrameDelay, ushort repeatCount = 0, params ISprite[] sprites) => sprites.AsEnumerable().AnimatedGif(frameDelay, repeatCount);
	public static Image<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32> AnimatedGif(this IEnumerable<ISprite> sprites, int frameDelay = DefaultFrameDelay, ushort repeatCount = 0)
	{
		Sprite[] resized = [.. sprites.SameSize()];
		Image<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32> gif = new(resized[0].Width, resized[0].Height);
		SixLabors.ImageSharp.Formats.Gif.GifMetadata gifMetaData = gif.Metadata.GetGifMetadata();
		gifMetaData.RepeatCount = repeatCount;
		gifMetaData.ColorTableMode = SixLabors.ImageSharp.Formats.FrameColorTableMode.Local;
		foreach (Sprite sprite in resized)
		{
			Image<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32> image = sprite.Png();
			SixLabors.ImageSharp.Formats.Gif.GifFrameMetadata metadata = image.Frames.RootFrame.Metadata.GetGifMetadata();
			metadata.FrameDelay = frameDelay;
			metadata.DisposalMode = SixLabors.ImageSharp.Formats.FrameDisposalMode.RestoreToBackground;
			gif.Frames.AddFrame(image.Frames.RootFrame);
		}
		gif.Frames.RemoveFrame(0);//I don't know why ImageSharp has me doing this but if I don't then I get an extra transparent frame at the start.
		return gif;
	}
	public static Image<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32> AnimatedGif(ushort width = 0, int frameDelay = DefaultFrameDelay, ushort repeatCount = 0, params byte[][] frames)
	{
		if (width < 1)
			width = (ushort)Math.Sqrt(frames[0].Length >> 2);
		int height = (frames[0].Length >> 2) / width;
		Image<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32> gif = new(width, height);
		SixLabors.ImageSharp.Formats.Gif.GifMetadata gifMetaData = gif.Metadata.GetGifMetadata();
		gifMetaData.RepeatCount = repeatCount;
		gifMetaData.ColorTableMode = SixLabors.ImageSharp.Formats.FrameColorTableMode.Local;
		foreach (byte[] frame in frames)
		{
			Image<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32> image = SixLabors.ImageSharp.Image.LoadPixelData<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32>(
				data: frame,
				width: width,
				height: height);
			SixLabors.ImageSharp.Formats.Gif.GifFrameMetadata metadata = image.Frames.RootFrame.Metadata.GetGifMetadata();
			metadata.FrameDelay = frameDelay;
			metadata.DisposalMode = SixLabors.ImageSharp.Formats.FrameDisposalMode.RestoreToBackground;
			gif.Frames.AddFrame(image.Frames.RootFrame);
		}
		gif.Frames.RemoveFrame(0);//I don't know why ImageSharp has me doing this but if I don't then I get an extra transparent frame at the start.
		return gif;
	}
	public static Image<SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats.Rgba32> AnimatedGif(byte scaleX = 1, byte scaleY = 1, ushort width = 0, int frameDelay = DefaultFrameDelay, ushort repeatCount = 0, params byte[][] frames) => AnimatedGif(
		width: (ushort)(width * scaleX),
		frameDelay: frameDelay,
		repeatCount: repeatCount,
		frames: scaleX == 1 && scaleY == 1 ? frames
			: [.. frames.Parallelize(frame => frame.Upscale(
			scaleX: scaleX,
			scaleY: scaleY,
			width: width))]);
	#endregion ImageSharp
}
```

### src/Voxel2Pixel/ExtensionMethods.cs
```
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Voxel2Pixel.Draw;
using Voxel2Pixel.Interfaces;
using Voxel2Pixel.Render;

namespace Voxel2Pixel;

public static class ExtensionMethods
{
	public static T Also<T>(this T obj, Action<T> action)
	{
		action(obj);
		return obj;
	}
	/// <param name="color">Using only colors 1-63</param>
	/// <returns>Big Endian RGBA8888 32-bit 256 color palette, leaving colors 0, 64, 128 and 192 as zeroes</returns>
	public static uint[] CreatePalette(this IVoxelColor color)
	{
		uint[] palette = new uint[256];
		foreach (VisibleFace face in Enum.GetValues(typeof(VisibleFace)))
			for (byte @byte = 1; @byte < 64; @byte++)
				palette[(byte)face + @byte] = color[@byte, face];
		return palette;
	}
	public static VisibleFace VisibleFace(this byte @byte) => (VisibleFace)(@byte & 192);
	public static bool IsInternallyScaled(this Perspective perspective, bool peak = false) => peak && (perspective == Perspective.Front || perspective == Perspective.Diagonal);
	public static bool IsCompound(this Perspective perspective) => perspective == Perspective.IsoEight || perspective == Perspective.IsoEightUnderneath;
	public static bool HasScaleZ(this Perspective perspective) => perspective == Perspective.Stacked;
	public static bool HasShadow(this Perspective perspective) => perspective == Perspective.Above || perspective == Perspective.Iso || perspective == Perspective.Stacked;
	#region PLINQ
	/// <summary>
	/// Parallelizes the execution of a Select query while preserving the order of the source sequence.
	/// </summary>
	public static List<TResult> Parallelize<TSource, TResult>(this IEnumerable<TSource> source, Func<TSource, TResult> selector) => [.. source
		.Select((element, index) => (element, index))
		.AsParallel()
		.Select(sourceTuple => (result: selector(sourceTuple.element), sourceTuple.index))
		.OrderBy(resultTuple => resultTuple.index)
		.AsEnumerable()
		.Select(resultTuple => resultTuple.result)];
	#endregion PLINQ
	#region Sprite
	public static Point Size(this ISprite sprite) => new(sprite.Width, sprite.Height);
	public static IEnumerable<Sprite> AddFrameNumbers(this IEnumerable<Sprite> frames, uint color = 0xFFFFFFFFu)
	{
		int frame = 0;
		foreach (Sprite sprite in frames)
		{
			sprite.Texture.Draw3x4(
				@string: (++frame).ToString(),
				width: sprite.Width,
				x: 0,
				y: sprite.Height - 4,
				color: color);
			yield return sprite;
		}
	}
	/// <summary>
	/// Warning: the new sprites only retain the Origin point, dropping all other points.
	/// </summary>
	public static IEnumerable<Sprite> SameSize(this IEnumerable<ISprite> sprites, ushort addWidth = 0, ushort addHeight = 0)
	{
		int originX = sprites.Select(sprite => sprite.TryGetValue(Sprite.Origin, out Point value) ? value.X : 0).Max(),
			originY = sprites.Select(sprite => sprite.TryGetValue(Sprite.Origin, out Point value) ? value.Y : 0).Max();
		ushort width = (ushort)(sprites.Select(sprite => sprite.Width + originX - (sprite.TryGetValue(Sprite.Origin, out Point value) ? value.X : 0)).Max() + addWidth),
			height = (ushort)(sprites.Select(sprite => sprite.Height + originY - (sprite.TryGetValue(Sprite.Origin, out Point value) ? value.Y : 0)).Max() + addHeight);
		foreach (ISprite sprite in sprites)
			yield return new Sprite
			{
				Texture = new byte[width * height << 2]
					.DrawInsert(
						x: originX - (sprite.TryGetValue(Sprite.Origin, out Point valueX) ? valueX.X : 0),
						y: originY - (sprite.TryGetValue(Sprite.Origin, out Point valueY) ? valueY.Y : 0),
						insert: sprite.Texture,
						insertWidth: sprite.Width,
						width: width),
				Width = width,
			}.SetRange(new KeyValuePair<string, Point>(Sprite.Origin, new Point(X: originX, Y: originY)));
	}
	#endregion Sprite
	#region SpriteMaker
	public static List<Sprite> Make(this IEnumerable<SpriteMaker> spriteMakers) => spriteMakers.Parallelize(spriteMaker => spriteMaker.Make());
	public static Dictionary<T, Sprite> Make<T>(this IDictionary<T, SpriteMaker> spriteMakers) => spriteMakers
		.AsParallel()
		.Select(spriteMakerPair => (
			key: spriteMakerPair.Key,
			sprite: spriteMakerPair.Value.Make()))
		.ToDictionary(//sequential
			keySelector: tuple => tuple.key,
			elementSelector: tuple => tuple.sprite);
	#endregion SpriteMaker
	#region Geometry
	public readonly record struct HorizontalLine(ushort X, ushort Width, ushort Y);
	public static IEnumerable<HorizontalLine> TriangleRows(Point a, Point b, Point c, Point? bounds = null)
	{
		ushort maxX = ushort.MaxValue;
		ushort maxY = ushort.MaxValue;
		if (bounds.HasValue)
		{
			if (bounds.Value.X < 1 || bounds.Value.Y < 1)
				throw new ArgumentException("Bounds must be at least 1x1", nameof(bounds));
			maxX = (ushort)(bounds.Value.X - 1);
			maxY = (ushort)(bounds.Value.Y - 1);
		}
		Point[] sortedPoints = [.. new[] { a, b, c }.OrderBy(point => point.Y)];
		double[] slopes = [
			CalculateSlope(sortedPoints[0], sortedPoints[2]),
			CalculateSlope(sortedPoints[0], sortedPoints[1]),
			CalculateSlope(sortedPoints[1], sortedPoints[2])];
		foreach (HorizontalLine line in GenerateLines(sortedPoints[0], sortedPoints[1], slopes[0], slopes[1], maxX, maxY))
			yield return line;
		foreach (HorizontalLine line in GenerateLines(sortedPoints[1], sortedPoints[2], slopes[0], slopes[2], maxX, maxY))
			yield return line;
	}
	public static double CalculateSlope(Point a, Point b) => a.X == b.X ?
		double.PositiveInfinity
		: (double)(b.Y - a.Y) / (b.X - a.X);
	private static IEnumerable<HorizontalLine> GenerateLines(Point start, Point end, double slope1, double slope2, ushort maxX, ushort maxY)
	{
		for (int y = Math.Max(0, start.Y); y <= Math.Min(end.Y, maxY); y++)
		{
			double x1 = start.X + (y - start.Y) / slope1;
			double x2 = start.X + (y - start.Y) / slope2;
			int startX = (int)Math.Ceiling(Math.Min(x1, x2));
			int endX = (int)Math.Floor(Math.Max(x1, x2));
			startX = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(startX, maxX));
			endX = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(endX, maxX));
			if (startX <= endX)
				yield return new HorizontalLine((ushort)startX, (ushort)(endX - startX + 1), (ushort)y);
		}
	}
	public static void DrawTriangle(this IRectangleRenderer renderer, uint color, Point a, Point b, Point c, Point? bounds = null)
	{
		foreach (HorizontalLine line in TriangleRows(a, b, c, bounds))
			renderer.Rect(
				x: line.X,
				y: line.Y,
				color: color,
				sizeX: (ushort)(line.Width + line.X));
	}
	#endregion Geometry
}
```

### src/Voxel2Pixel/Records.cs

using BenVoxel.Structs;

namespace Voxel2Pixel;

public readonly record struct Point(int X, int Y)
{
	public override string ToString() => $"[{X},{Y}]";
	public Point(Point3D point3d, ushort sizeY = 0) : this(
		X: point3d.X,
		Y: sizeY == 0 ? point3d.Y : sizeY - 1 - point3d.Y)
	{ }
}
public enum VisibleFace : byte
{
	Front = 0,
	Top = 64,
	Left = 128,
	Right = 192,
}
public enum Perspective
{//May need revision to follow the correct terms shown in this graphic: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Comparison_of_graphical_projections.svg
	Front, Overhead, Underneath, Diagonal, Above, Iso, IsoUnderneath, IsoEight, IsoEightUnderneath, Stacked, StackedUnderneath, ZSlices,
}

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Interaction/Hand.cs
```
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

/// <summary>
/// One tracked hand. Wraps a controller so nothing else reads buttons or poses from the controller directly:
/// a different input source (for example optical hand tracking) could replace this class without touching
/// anything that interacts with it. <see cref="Update"/> must be called once per frame, before reading state.
/// </summary>
public sealed class Hand(XRController3D controller)
{
	/// <summary>Distance in metres from the grip pose to the controller tip (the marker box is 0.1 m deep, centred on the grip).</summary>
	public const float TipOffset = 0.05f;

	public XRController3D Controller { get; } = controller;
	public Transform3D GlobalTransform => Controller.GlobalTransform;
	/// <summary>World position of the point this hand touches things with.</summary>
	public Vector3 TipWorld => Controller.GlobalTransform * new Vector3(0f, 0f, -TipOffset);
	public bool HasTracking => Controller.GetHasTrackingData();
	public bool GripPressed { get; private set; }
	public bool TriggerPressed { get; private set; }
	/// <summary>True only on the frame the trigger went down.</summary>
	public bool TriggerJustPressed { get; private set; }
	/// <summary>The A/X face button, held.</summary>
	public bool AxPressed { get; private set; }
	/// <summary>Thumbstick deflection, each axis -1 to 1: x right, y up.</summary>
	public Vector2 Stick { get; private set; }
	public bool StickPressed { get; private set; }
	/// <summary>True only on the frame the thumbstick was clicked down.</summary>
	public bool StickJustPressed { get; private set; }
	/// <summary>True only on the frame the thumbstick click let go.</summary>
	public bool StickJustReleased { get; private set; }
	/// <summary>What this hand is currently pointing at, as resolved by <see cref="InteractionManager"/>; null if nothing.</summary>
	public IInteractable Hovered { get; internal set; }

	/// <summary>Buzzes the controller. Amplitude is 0 to 1.</summary>
	public void Pulse(float amplitude, float seconds) => Controller.TriggerHapticPulse("haptic", 0f, amplitude, seconds, 0f);

	public void Update()
	{
		GripPressed = Controller.IsButtonPressed("grip_click");
		bool trigger = Controller.IsButtonPressed("trigger_click");
		TriggerJustPressed = trigger && !TriggerPressed;
		TriggerPressed = trigger;
		AxPressed = Controller.IsButtonPressed("ax_button");
		Stick = Controller.GetVector2("primary");
		bool stick = Controller.IsButtonPressed("primary_click");
		StickJustPressed = stick && !StickPressed;
		StickJustReleased = !stick && StickPressed;
		StickPressed = stick;
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Interaction/IInteractable.cs
```
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

/// <summary>What a grip does to an interactable.</summary>
public enum GrabMode
{
	/// <summary>Grip does nothing.</summary>
	None,
	/// <summary>One hand drags the node's position; it keeps its orientation and scale.</summary>
	Drag,
	/// <summary>One hand carries the node (position and orientation); two hands also scale it.</summary>
	Free,
}

/// <summary>
/// Anything a hand can touch. Register it with <see cref="InteractionManager"/>, which resolves what each hand
/// is hovering, delivers hover and trigger events, and runs grabs according to <see cref="Grab"/>.
/// </summary>
public interface IInteractable
{
	/// <summary>The node moved by grabs.</summary>
	Node3D Node { get; }
	GrabMode Grab { get; }
	/// <summary>When several interactables are touched at once, the highest priority wins; the nearer wins a tie.</summary>
	int Priority { get; }
	/// <summary>True if the voxel edit cursor should hide while a hand hovers this.</summary>
	bool SuppressesEditCursor => false;
	/// <summary>Distance in metres at which the hand touches this, or null if it does not. Used to rank hits.</summary>
	float? HitTest(Hand hand);
	void HoverEnter(Hand hand) { }
	void HoverExit(Hand hand) { }
	void TriggerDown(Hand hand) { }
	/// <summary>
	/// Called when a grip is about to carry this. Returns what to carry instead, which lets an item that only stands
	/// for something (an icon in the toy box) bring the real thing into being under the hand. It must already be
	/// registered with the <see cref="InteractionManager"/>, and be in the same <see cref="Grab"/> mode.
	/// </summary>
	IInteractable BeginGrab(Hand hand) => this;
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Interaction/InteractionManager.cs
```
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

/// <summary>
/// Owns everything that happens between the hands and the things in the scene. Each frame, <see cref="Update"/>:
/// resolves what each hand is hovering (sending hover events), delivers trigger presses to the hovered item, and runs
/// grip grabs according to the grabbed item's <see cref="IInteractable.Grab"/> mode.
///
/// Hovering: the highest <see cref="IInteractable.Priority"/> hit wins, the nearest on a tie, and the earlier
/// registered one if still tied. While a hand holds a grab, it keeps hovering what it grabbed.
///
/// Grabbing: <see cref="GrabMode.Drag"/> starts when exactly one grip goes down on a hovered item and ignores the other
/// hand until it is released. Otherwise a <see cref="GrabMode.Free"/> item is picked by the hand that gripped (the
/// primary hand if both did), or the fallback if it touches none, and stays the same until all grips are released.
/// While one grip is held the item follows that controller (position and orientation). While both are held it follows
/// the midpoint between them, is oriented by the line between the hands (with roll taken from the controllers' up
/// directions) and is scaled by the change in hand distance. All cases use one "grab frame" whose basis is scaled by
/// the hand distance; the item is moved by frame_now * frame_start^-1, so it stays rigidly attached to the frame.
/// Pressing or releasing a grip changes the frame's definition, so the grab is re-baselined at that moment to avoid
/// a jump.
/// </summary>
public sealed class InteractionManager(Hand left, Hand right)
{
	private readonly List<IInteractable> _items = [];
	private IInteractable _fallback;

	public Hand Left { get; } = left;
	public Hand Right { get; } = right;
	/// <summary>The dominant hand: the one that edits, and the one that picks a Free grab when both grip at once.</summary>
	public Hand Primary => Right;

	/// <summary>Which hands were gripping on the previous frame (bit 0 left, bit 1 right); a change re-baselines the grab.</summary>
	private int _gripMask;
	/// <summary>The item being carried by a Free grab; null when none.</summary>
	private IInteractable _grabTarget;
	/// <summary>Grab frame and item transform captured when the current Free grab was last re-baselined.</summary>
	private Transform3D _grabFrameStart;
	private Transform3D _targetStart;
	/// <summary>The item and hand of the current Drag, and the item's position in that hand's local space when it started.</summary>
	private IInteractable _dragTarget;
	private Hand _dragHand;
	private Vector3 _dragOffset;
	/// <summary>The hand last gripping a Free grab, kept so a release can say which hand let go.</summary>
	private Hand _lastGripHand;

	/// <summary>Adds an item to be hovered and grabbed. Registration order breaks ties between equally near items.</summary>
	/// <param name="fallback">If true, a Free grab that touches nothing carries this item instead.</param>
	public void Register(IInteractable item, bool fallback = false)
	{
		_items.Add(item);
		if (fallback)
			_fallback = item;
	}

	/// <summary>Removes an item; any hand hovering it stops hovering it. Do not call during a grab of the item.</summary>
	public void Unregister(IInteractable item)
	{
		_items.Remove(item);
		if (_fallback == item)
			_fallback = null;
		foreach (Hand hand in (Hand[])[Left, Right])
		{
			if (hand.Hovered == item)
				SetHovered(hand, null);
		}
	}

	/// <summary>Raised when the last grip carrying an item lets go, with the item and the hand that let go last.</summary>
	public event System.Action<IInteractable, Hand> Released;

	public void Update()
	{
		Left.Update();
		Right.Update();
		UpdateHover(Left);
		UpdateHover(Right);
		UpdateTriggers(Left);
		UpdateTriggers(Right);
		UpdateGrab();
	}

	private void UpdateHover(Hand hand)
	{
		IInteractable hovered;
		if (_dragHand == hand)
			hovered = _dragTarget;
		else if (_grabTarget is not null && hand.GripPressed)
			hovered = _grabTarget;
		else
			hovered = Pick(hand, freeOnly: false);
		SetHovered(hand, hovered);
	}

	private static void SetHovered(Hand hand, IInteractable hovered)
	{
		if (hand.Hovered == hovered)
			return;
		hand.Hovered?.HoverExit(hand);
		hand.Hovered = hovered;
		hovered?.HoverEnter(hand);
	}

	private static void UpdateTriggers(Hand hand)
	{
		if (hand.TriggerJustPressed)
			hand.Hovered?.TriggerDown(hand);
	}

	private IInteractable Pick(Hand hand, bool freeOnly)
	{
		IInteractable best = null;
		float bestDistance = 0f;
		foreach (IInteractable item in _items)
		{
			if (freeOnly && item.Grab != GrabMode.Free)
				continue;
			if (item.HitTest(hand) is not float distance)
				continue;
			if (best is null || item.Priority > best.Priority || (item.Priority == best.Priority && distance < bestDistance))
			{
				best = item;
				bestDistance = distance;
			}
		}
		return best;
	}

	private void UpdateGrab()
	{
		bool l = Left.GripPressed, r = Right.GripPressed;
		if (UpdateDrag(l, r))
			return;
		int mask = (l ? 1 : 0) | (r ? 2 : 0);
		if (mask == 0)
		{
			IInteractable released = _grabTarget;
			_gripMask = 0;
			_grabTarget = null;
			if (released is not null)
				Released?.Invoke(released, _lastGripHand);
			return;
		}
		_lastGripHand = r ? Right : Left;
		if (_gripMask == 0)
		{
			// Both grips down at once means the primary hand chooses, and only Free items can be chosen
			Hand picker = r ? Right : Left;
			_grabTarget = Pick(picker, freeOnly: true)?.BeginGrab(picker) ?? _fallback;
			if (_grabTarget is null)
				return;
		}
		Transform3D frame = GetGrabFrame(l, r);
		if (mask != _gripMask)
		{
			_gripMask = mask;
			_grabFrameStart = frame;
			_targetStart = _grabTarget.Node.GlobalTransform;
			return;
		}
		_grabTarget.Node.GlobalTransform = frame * _grabFrameStart.AffineInverse() * _targetStart;
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Runs Drag grabs, which take priority over Free grabs. Returns true if the Free grab should be skipped this
	/// frame. While dragging, the Free grab is suspended and its state reset, so a still-held other grip
	/// re-baselines cleanly afterwards.
	/// </summary>
	private bool UpdateDrag(bool l, bool r)
	{
		if (_dragHand is not null && !_dragHand.GripPressed)
		{
			IInteractable released = _dragTarget;
			Hand hand = _dragHand;
			_dragHand = null;
			_dragTarget = null;
			Released?.Invoke(released, hand);
		}
		else if (_dragHand is null && _gripMask == 0 && l != r)
		{
			Hand hand = r ? Right : Left;
			if (hand.Hovered is { Grab: GrabMode.Drag } target)
			{
				target = target.BeginGrab(hand);
				_dragHand = hand;
				_dragTarget = target;
				_dragOffset = hand.GlobalTransform.AffineInverse() * target.Node.GlobalPosition;
			}
		}
		if (_dragHand is null)
			return false;
		_gripMask = 0;
		_grabTarget = null;
		_dragTarget.Node.GlobalPosition = _dragHand.GlobalTransform * _dragOffset;
		return true;
	}

	private Transform3D GetGrabFrame(bool l, bool r)
	{
		if (!(l && r))
			return (l ? Left : Right).GlobalTransform;
		Transform3D lt = Left.GlobalTransform;
		Transform3D rt = Right.GlobalTransform;
		Vector3 across = rt.Origin - lt.Origin;
		float distance = Mathf.Max(across.Length(), 0.01f);
		Vector3 x = across / distance;
		Vector3 up = lt.Basis.Y + rt.Basis.Y;
		Vector3 z = x.Cross(up);
		if (z.LengthSquared() < 1e-6f)
			z = x.Cross(Vector3.Up); // controllers' up is parallel to the hand line; any perpendicular will do
		z = z.Normalized();
		Vector3 y = z.Cross(x);
		return new Transform3D(new Basis(x, y, z).Scaled(Vector3.One * distance), (lt.Origin + rt.Origin) / 2);
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Interaction/MiniIcon.cs
```
using BenVoxel.VR.Props;
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

/// <summary>Makes miniature icons out of arbitrary 3D objects; shared by every menu that shows them.</summary>
public static class MiniIcon
{
	/// <summary>
	/// Wraps an object so that it is centred on the wrapper's origin and scaled so the diagonal of its bounds is
	/// <paramref name="diagonal"/> metres, whatever its own size and origin.
	/// </summary>
	public static Node3D Fit(Node3D icon, float diagonal)
	{
		Aabb bounds = ModelProp.MeshBounds(icon, Transform3D.Identity) ?? new Aabb(Vector3.Zero, Vector3.One);
		float scale = diagonal / Mathf.Max(bounds.Size.Length(), 0.0001f);
		Node3D fit = new() { Name = "Fit", Scale = Vector3.One * scale, Position = -bounds.GetCenter() * scale };
		fit.AddChild(icon);
		return fit;
	}

	/// <summary>A small white sphere standing for a light.</summary>
	public static MeshInstance3D LightSphere(float radius) => new()
	{
		Name = "LightIcon",
		Mesh = new SphereMesh { Radius = radius, Height = radius * 2f },
		MaterialOverride = new StandardMaterial3D { ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded, AlbedoColor = Colors.White },
		CastShadow = GeometryInstance3D.ShadowCastingSetting.Off,
	};
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Interaction/RadialMenu.cs
```
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

/// <summary>
/// A ring of floating 3D objects, one per item, driven by a hand's thumbstick: pushing the stick out of its dead zone
/// opens the menu, the item the stick points at is picked out, and clicking the stick chooses it. Letting the stick
/// return to its dead zone closes the menu without choosing anything.
///
/// Nothing here depends on how many items there are. Each item owns an equal sector of the circle (a full turn divided
/// by the item count), the first straight up and the rest clockwise, and the ring is made just wide enough that
/// neighbouring icons do not touch, so adding an item with <see cref="AddItem"/> re-lays-out the whole ring.
///
/// The menu stays attached to the hand that opened it, a little toward the viewer and always facing them, so that stick
/// up is always up for the viewer. A dot shows where the stick is, and only the picked icon turns.
///
/// How a menu is opened, picked from and closed lives in <see cref="Update"/> alone, so a menu that opens another way
/// (for example a per-tool menu opened by a stick click) can differ from this one without touching the layout. Call <see cref="Update"/> once per frame for each hand after <see cref="Hand.Update"/>.
/// </summary>
public sealed partial class RadialMenu : Node3D
{
	/// <summary>Length in metres of the diagonal of the box that every icon is scaled to fit, so any model works as an icon.</summary>
	private const float IconDiagonal = 0.045f;
	/// <summary>Least distance between neighbouring icon centres, in icon diagonals. It leaves room for the picked icon to grow.</summary>
	private const float Spacing = 1.6f;
	/// <summary>Ring radius in metres when there are so few items that spacing alone would give a tiny ring.</summary>
	private const float MinRadius = 0.05f;
	/// <summary>How much bigger the picked icon is drawn.</summary>
	private const float PickedScale = 1.4f;
	/// <summary>Stick deflection (0 to 1) that opens the menu.</summary>
	private const float OpenDeadZone = 0.6f;
	/// <summary>Stick deflection below which an open menu closes and nothing is picked; lower than <see cref="OpenDeadZone"/> so a stick hovering at the edge does not flicker the menu.</summary>
	private const float CloseDeadZone = 0.4f;
	/// <summary>How far in metres toward the viewer from the hand the ring's centre opens.</summary>
	private const float OpenDistance = 0.06f;
	private const float SpinRadiansPerSecond = 2.5f;
	/// <summary>Radius in metres of the dot that shows where the stick is.</summary>
	private const float DotRadius = 0.005f;
	/// <summary>Higher is snappier growing, shrinking and turning back of icons.</summary>
	private const float ScaleRate = 15f;

	private sealed class Item(string name, Node3D slot, Node3D spinner, Action<Hand> chosen)
	{
		public string Name { get; } = name;
		/// <summary>Placed on the ring and scaled when picked.</summary>
		public Node3D Slot { get; } = slot;
		/// <summary>Turns the icon about the vertical so its shape can be seen, but only while it is picked.</summary>
		public Node3D Spinner { get; } = spinner;
		/// <summary>Current turn of <see cref="Spinner"/> in radians.</summary>
		public float Angle { get; set; }
		public Action<Hand> Chosen { get; } = chosen;
	}

	private readonly List<Item> _items = [];
	private readonly Label3D _label = new()
	{
		Name = "Label",
		Billboard = BaseMaterial3D.BillboardModeEnum.Enabled,
		NoDepthTest = true,
		PixelSize = 0.00015f,
		FontSize = 64,
		OutlineSize = 16,
	};
	/// <summary>The hand that opened the menu; null when closed.</summary>
	private Hand _owner;
	/// <summary>A hand that has just chosen an item and so cannot reopen the menu until its stick returns to the dead zone; null if none.</summary>
	private Hand _awaitingNeutral;
	/// <summary>Index of the picked item, or -1.</summary>
	private int _picked = -1;
	/// <summary>Radius of the ring in metres, as laid out for the current item count.</summary>
	private float _radius = MinRadius;
	/// <summary>Shows where the thumbstick is, on the same circle the icons are on.</summary>
	private readonly MeshInstance3D _dot = new()
	{
		Name = "StickDot",
		Mesh = new SphereMesh { Radius = DotRadius, Height = DotRadius * 2f },
		MaterialOverride = new StandardMaterial3D
		{
			ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded,
			AlbedoColor = Colors.White,
			NoDepthTest = true,
		},
		CastShadow = GeometryInstance3D.ShadowCastingSetting.Off,
	};

	public bool IsOpen => _owner is not null;

	public RadialMenu()
	{
		Visible = false;
		AddChild(_label);
		AddChild(_dot);
	}

	/// <summary>
	/// Adds an item to the end of the ring (the next sector clockwise). The icon is measured, centred and scaled to fit
	/// whatever its own size and origin, and is only displayed: it should not be registered with the interaction manager.
	/// </summary>
	/// <param name="tiltDegrees">Leans the icon over by this much, so that turning it about the vertical shows something even for an upright object.</param>
	/// <param name="chosen">Called with the hand that opened the menu when the item is chosen.</param>
	public void AddItem(string name, Node3D icon, Action<Hand> chosen, float tiltDegrees = 0f)
	{
		Node3D fit = MiniIcon.Fit(icon, IconDiagonal);
		Node3D tilt = new() { Name = "Tilt", Rotation = new Vector3(0f, 0f, Mathf.DegToRad(tiltDegrees)) };
		tilt.AddChild(fit);
		Node3D spinner = new() { Name = "Spinner" };
		spinner.AddChild(tilt);
		Node3D slot = new() { Name = name };
		slot.AddChild(spinner);
		AddChild(slot);
		_items.Add(new Item(name, slot, spinner, chosen));
		Layout();
	}

	/// <summary>Gives each item an equal sector and puts its icon on the ring through the sector's centre.</summary>
	private void Layout()
	{
		float sector = Mathf.Tau / _items.Count;
		// Neighbouring centres are 2 * radius * sin(sector / 2) apart; a lone item has no neighbour to keep clear of
		_radius = _items.Count < 2
			? MinRadius
			: Mathf.Max(MinRadius, IconDiagonal * Spacing / (2f * Mathf.Sin(sector / 2f)));
		for (int i = 0; i < _items.Count; i++)
		{
			float angle = i * sector;
			_items[i].Slot.Position = new Vector3(Mathf.Sin(angle), Mathf.Cos(angle), 0f) * _radius;
		}
	}

	/// <summary>Opens, moves with and closes the menu according to one hand's thumbstick.</summary>
	/// <param name="headPosition">The viewer's position in world space; the menu opens facing it.</param>
	public void Update(Hand hand, Vector3 headPosition)
	{
		float deflection = hand.Stick.Length();
		if (_owner is null)
		{
			if (_awaitingNeutral == hand && deflection < CloseDeadZone)
				_awaitingNeutral = null;
			if (_awaitingNeutral != hand && deflection >= OpenDeadZone && _items.Count > 0)
				Open(hand, headPosition);
			return;
		}
		if (_owner != hand)
			return;
		// A click jolts the stick, so it chooses what was picked on the frame before, not what is under the stick now
		if (hand.StickJustPressed && _picked >= 0)
		{
			Close(choose: true);
			return;
		}
		if (deflection < CloseDeadZone)
		{
			Close(choose: false);
			return;
		}
		Place(hand, headPosition);
		Pick(PickSector(hand.Stick));
		_dot.Position = new Vector3(hand.Stick.X, hand.Stick.Y, 0f).LimitLength() * _radius;
	}

	/// <summary>Puts the menu a little toward the viewer from the hand. -Z looks away from the viewer, so the menu's front (+Z) faces them, with X right and Y up for them.</summary>
	private void Place(Hand hand, Vector3 headPosition)
	{
		Vector3 origin = hand.GlobalTransform.Origin;
		Vector3 towardHead = (headPosition - origin).Normalized();
		GlobalTransform = new Transform3D(Basis.LookingAt(-towardHead, Vector3.Up), origin + towardHead * OpenDistance);
	}

	private void Open(Hand hand, Vector3 headPosition)
	{
		_owner = hand;
		Place(hand, headPosition);
		Pick(PickSector(hand.Stick));
		_dot.Position = new Vector3(hand.Stick.X, hand.Stick.Y, 0f).LimitLength() * _radius;
		Visible = true;
	}

	private void Close(bool choose)
	{
		Hand owner = _owner;
		int chosen = _picked;
		_owner = null;
		Visible = false;
		Pick(-1);
		if (!choose || chosen < 0)
			return;
		_awaitingNeutral = owner;
		_items[chosen].Chosen(owner);
	}

	/// <summary>The item the stick points at, or -1 if it is too near the centre.</summary>
	private int PickSector(Vector2 stick)
	{
		if (stick.Length() < CloseDeadZone)
			return -1;
		// Clockwise from straight up, the way the items are laid out
		float angle = Mathf.PosMod(Mathf.Atan2(stick.X, stick.Y), Mathf.Tau);
		float sector = Mathf.Tau / _items.Count;
		return (int)Mathf.Round(angle / sector) % _items.Count;
	}

	private void Pick(int index)
	{
		_picked = index;
		_label.Text = index >= 0 ? _items[index].Name : "";
	}

	public override void _Process(double delta)
	{
		if (_owner is null)
			return;
		float blend = 1f - Mathf.Exp(-ScaleRate * (float)delta);
		for (int i = 0; i < _items.Count; i++)
		{
			Vector3 target = Vector3.One * (i == _picked ? PickedScale : 1f);
			_items[i].Slot.Scale = _items[i].Slot.Scale.Lerp(target, blend);
			// Turning is the reward for picking: the picked icon spins, and the others settle back to rest
			Item item = _items[i];
			item.Angle = i == _picked ? item.Angle + SpinRadiansPerSecond * (float)delta : Mathf.LerpAngle(item.Angle, 0f, blend);
			item.Spinner.Rotation = new Vector3(0f, item.Angle, 0f);
		}
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Interaction/WireframeCube.cs
```
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR;

/// <summary>A unit cube drawn as its twelve edges only, centred on the node. The select tool's icon.</summary>
public partial class WireframeCube : MeshInstance3D
{
	public WireframeCube()
	{
		Name = "WireframeCube";
		Vector3[] corners = new Vector3[8];
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
			corners[i] = new Vector3((i & 1) - 0.5f, ((i >> 1) & 1) - 0.5f, ((i >> 2) & 1) - 0.5f);
		// An edge joins two corners that differ in exactly one bit of their index
		System.Collections.Generic.List<int> edges = [];
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
		{
			for (int bit = 1; bit <= 4; bit <<= 1)
			{
				if ((i & bit) == 0)
				{
					edges.Add(i);
					edges.Add(i | bit);
				}
			}
		}
		Godot.Collections.Array arrays = [];
		arrays.Resize((int)Mesh.ArrayType.Max);
		arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Vertex] = corners;
		arrays[(int)Mesh.ArrayType.Index] = edges.ToArray();
		ArrayMesh mesh = new();
		mesh.AddSurfaceFromArrays(Mesh.PrimitiveType.Lines, arrays);
		Mesh = mesh;
		MaterialOverride = new StandardMaterial3D
		{
			ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded,
			AlbedoColor = Colors.White,
		};
		CastShadow = GeometryInstance3D.ShadowCastingSetting.Off;
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Props/LightProp.cs
```
using System;
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR.Props;

/// <summary>
/// The point light, shown as a white sphere in world space (a child of the root, so grab-scaling the model does not
/// resize it). Dragging it with a grip moves it; hovering shows a halo; the trigger toggles it. When off (black) the
/// viewer's head is the light source instead (see <see cref="Root"/>).
/// </summary>
public sealed class LightProp : IInteractable
{
	/// <summary>Radius of the light sphere in metres.</summary>
	public const float Radius = 0.025f;

	private readonly Func<Vector3, Vector3, float, bool> _cellTouchesSphere;
	private readonly StandardMaterial3D _material;
	private readonly MeshInstance3D _sphere;
	private readonly MeshInstance3D _halo;
	/// <summary>Number of hands hovering; the halo stays up while any does.</summary>
	private int _hoverCount;

	/// <param name="cellTouchesSphere">
	/// Given a hand tip, a sphere centre and a radius (all world space, metres), says whether the voxel cell under the
	/// tip overlaps the sphere. The light is touched when the hand's edit cursor would be touching it, which is how
	/// the cursor's blinking cube tells you it is on the light.
	/// </param>
	public LightProp(Vector3 worldPosition, Func<Vector3, Vector3, float, bool> cellTouchesSphere)
	{
		_cellTouchesSphere = cellTouchesSphere;
		_material = new StandardMaterial3D
		{
			ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded,
			AlbedoColor = Colors.White,
		};
		_sphere = new MeshInstance3D
		{
			Name = "LightSphere",
			Mesh = new SphereMesh { Radius = Radius, Height = Radius * 2f },
			MaterialOverride = _material,
			CastShadow = GeometryInstance3D.ShadowCastingSetting.Off,
			Position = worldPosition,
		};
		_halo = new MeshInstance3D
		{
			Name = "LightHalo",
			Mesh = new SphereMesh { Radius = Radius * 2f, Height = Radius * 4f },
			MaterialOverride = new StandardMaterial3D
			{
				ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded,
				Transparency = BaseMaterial3D.TransparencyEnum.Alpha,
				BlendMode = BaseMaterial3D.BlendModeEnum.Add,
				AlbedoColor = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.25f),
			},
			CastShadow = GeometryInstance3D.ShadowCastingSetting.Off,
			Visible = false,
		};
		_sphere.AddChild(_halo);
	}

	public Node3D Node => _sphere;
	/// <summary>True when the light is on (white).</summary>
	public bool On { get; private set; } = true;
	public Vector3 WorldPosition => _sphere.GlobalPosition;

	public GrabMode Grab => GrabMode.Drag;
	/// <summary>Above the model and sprite, which are touched by much larger volumes.</summary>
	public int Priority => 1;
	public bool SuppressesEditCursor => true;

	public float? HitTest(Hand hand) =>
		hand.HasTracking && _cellTouchesSphere(hand.TipWorld, _sphere.GlobalPosition, Radius)
			? hand.TipWorld.DistanceTo(_sphere.GlobalPosition)
			: null;

	public void HoverEnter(Hand hand) => _halo.Visible = ++_hoverCount > 0;

	public void HoverExit(Hand hand) => _halo.Visible = --_hoverCount > 0;

	public void TriggerDown(Hand hand)
	{
		On = !On;
		_material.AlbedoColor = On ? Colors.White : Colors.Black;
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Props/ModelProp.cs
```
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR.Props;

/// <summary>
/// A prop showing an imported 3D model (see assets/ASSETS.md for where each came from). Whatever the model's
/// own size, units and orientation, it is measured when loaded, turned so its longest dimension points up, scaled to
/// <c>longestMetres</c> and centred on this node, so it is grabbed and scaled like any other prop. It can spin slowly
/// about the vertical so it can be seen from all sides.
/// </summary>
public partial class ModelProp : Prop
{
	private readonly Node3D _spinner = new() { Name = "Spinner" };
	private readonly float _spinRadiansPerSecond;
	private readonly float _radius;

	public override (Vector3 Centre, float Radius) LocalSphere => (Vector3.Zero, _radius);

	public ModelProp(string scenePath, float longestMetres, float spinDegreesPerSecond = 0f)
	{
		Name = System.IO.Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(scenePath);
		_spinRadiansPerSecond = Mathf.DegToRad(spinDegreesPerSecond);
		_radius = longestMetres * 0.6f;
		Node3D model = GD.Load<PackedScene>(scenePath).Instantiate<Node3D>();
		Aabb bounds = MeshBounds(model, Transform3D.Identity) ?? new Aabb(Vector3.Zero, Vector3.One);
		Vector3 size = bounds.Size;
		// Turn the longest axis to point up
		Basis upright = size.X >= size.Y && size.X >= size.Z ? new Basis(Vector3.Back, Mathf.Pi / 2f)
			: size.Z >= size.Y && size.Z >= size.X ? new Basis(Vector3.Right, -Mathf.Pi / 2f)
			: Basis.Identity;
		float scale = longestMetres / Mathf.Max(size.X, Mathf.Max(size.Y, size.Z));
		Node3D aligned = new()
		{
			Name = "Aligned",
			Basis = upright.Scaled(Vector3.One * scale),
		};
		model.Position = -bounds.GetCenter();
		aligned.AddChild(model);
		_spinner.AddChild(aligned);
		AddChild(_spinner);
		// Nothing in the scene is lit but the voxels, whose shader does its own lighting, so show textures as they are
		foreach (Node child in model.FindChildren("*", nameof(MeshInstance3D), true, false))
		{
			MeshInstance3D mesh = (MeshInstance3D)child;
			for (int i = 0; i < mesh.GetSurfaceOverrideMaterialCount(); i++)
			{
				if (mesh.GetActiveMaterial(i) is BaseMaterial3D source)
				{
					BaseMaterial3D material = (BaseMaterial3D)source.Duplicate();
					material.ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded;
					// Anisotropic, so steeply angled surfaces like the pencil's tip stay sharp instead of smearing colours across edges
					material.TextureFilter = BaseMaterial3D.TextureFilterEnum.LinearWithMipmapsAnisotropic;
					mesh.SetSurfaceOverrideMaterial(i, material);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	public override void _Process(double delta) => _spinner.RotateY(_spinRadiansPerSecond * (float)delta);

	/// <summary>The bounds of every mesh under a node, in the space of the node's parent chain given by <paramref name="toRoot"/>.</summary>
	internal static Aabb? MeshBounds(Node3D node, Transform3D toRoot)
	{
		Transform3D transform = toRoot * node.Transform;
		Aabb? result = null;
		if (node is MeshInstance3D mesh && mesh.Mesh is not null)
			result = transform * mesh.GetAabb();
		foreach (Node child in node.GetChildren())
		{
			if (child is Node3D childNode && MeshBounds(childNode, transform) is Aabb childBounds)
				result = result is Aabb existing ? existing.Merge(childBounds) : childBounds;
		}
		return result;
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Props/Prop.cs
```
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR.Props;

/// <summary>
/// Base class for every placeable object in the scene, including the models. A prop is automatically touched,
/// hovered and carried by hands: one hand moves and turns it, two hands also scale it (see <see cref="InteractionManager"/>).
/// A prop only says how big it is (<see cref="LocalSphere"/>); add it to the scene with <see cref="Root.AddProp"/>, which
/// also makes it grabbable, so none of that is written again for each prop.
/// </summary>
public abstract partial class Prop : Node3D, IInteractable
{
	/// <summary>The sphere a hand has to be inside to touch this prop, in the prop's local space (it follows the prop's scale).</summary>
	public abstract (Vector3 Centre, float Radius) LocalSphere { get; }

	/// <summary>Higher wins where props overlap; <see cref="VoxelProp"/> lowers this because it is touched by a much larger volume.</summary>
	public virtual int Priority => 1;

	Node3D IInteractable.Node => this;
	public GrabMode Grab => GrabMode.Free;

	public virtual void HoverEnter(Hand hand) { }
	public virtual void HoverExit(Hand hand) { }
	public virtual void TriggerDown(Hand hand) { }

	public virtual float? HitTest(Hand hand)
	{
		(Vector3 centre, float radius) = LocalSphere;
		float distance = ToGlobal(centre).DistanceTo(hand.TipWorld);
		return distance <= radius * GlobalTransform.Basis.Scale.X ? distance : null;
	}
}
```

### godot/BenVoxel.VR/Props/ToyBox.cs
```
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Godot;

namespace BenVoxel.VR.Props;

/// <summary>
/// The toy box: a treasure chest (assets/chest/chest.glb, see assets/ASSETS.md). The node is the chest's pivot, at the
/// centre of its base, in metres. The model is a child scaled to
/// about <see cref="WidthMetres"/> wide.
///
/// A trigger on the chest opens or closes a grid of miniature icons floating above it, one per entry added with
/// <see cref="AddItem"/>. The grid wraps at <see cref="Columns"/> and grows upward, so an entry is added with one call
/// and nothing else changes. Gripping an icon brings the real prop into being under the hand at full size; letting go
/// of a prop that came from here with the hand on the chest puts it away again.
/// </summary>
public partial class ToyBox : Prop
{
	/// <summary>Chest width in metres.</summary>
	private const float WidthMetres = 0.14f;
	/// <summary>The model's width in its own units, and how far its base is below the model origin (both measured from the glb).</summary>
	private const float ModelWidth = 1.6446f * 1.7780f;
	private const float ModelBaseBelowOrigin = 0.4412f * 1.7780f;

	/// <summary>
	/// How far the model is turned about the vertical (counter-clockwise seen from above) so that its front faces this node's +Z,
	/// which is what <c>Root.SummonToyBox</c> relies on. The glb's own front faces a different way.
	/// </summary>
	private static readonly float FrontTurn = Mathf.DegToRad(90f);

	/// <summary>Icons per row of the grid.</summary>
	private const int Columns = 4;
	/// <summary>Distance in metres between neighbouring icon centres.</summary>
	private const float Cell = 0.09f;
	/// <summary>Length in metres of the diagonal of the box every icon is scaled to fit.</summary>
	private const float IconDiagonal = 0.06f;
	/// <summary>Height in metres of the grid's bottom row above the chest's base.</summary>
	private const float GridBase = 0.17f;
	/// <summary>Strength and length in seconds of the buzz when a hand moves onto an icon, and when it pulls a prop out.</summary>
	private const float HoverPulse = 0.25f, HoverPulseSeconds = 0.04f;
	private const float SpawnPulse = 0.7f, SpawnPulseSeconds = 0.1f;
	/// <summary>Buzz on touching the chest and on clicking it.</summary>
	private const float TouchPulse = 0.35f, TouchPulseSeconds = 0.05f;
	private const float ClickPulse = 0.6f, ClickPulseSeconds = 0.08f;
	private const float HoveredScale = 1.4f;
	private const float SpinRadiansPerSecond = 2.5f;
	private const float ScaleRate = 15f;

	/// <summary>What one icon in the grid stands for.</summary>
	/// <param name="Spawn">Brings the real thing into the world under the hand, registered for interaction, and returns it. It may return something that already exists.</param>
	/// <param name="Despawn">Removes something that <paramref name="Spawn"/> returned from the world.</param>
	private sealed record Entry(string Name, GrabMode Grab, Func<Hand, IInteractable> Spawn, Action<IInteractable> Despawn);

	private sealed class Slot(ToyBox owner, Entry entry, Node3D node, Node3D spinner) : IInteractable
	{
		public Node3D Node { get; } = node;
		public Node3D Spinner { get; } = spinner;
		public float Angle { get; set; }
		public int Hovers { get; private set; }
		public GrabMode Grab => entry.Grab;
		/// <summary>Above the chest itself, whose touch sphere the grid overlaps.</summary>
		public int Priority => 2;
		public bool SuppressesEditCursor => true;

		public float? HitTest(Hand hand)
		{
			if (!owner._open || !hand.HasTracking)
				return null;
			float distance = Node.GlobalPosition.DistanceTo(hand.TipWorld);
			return distance <= Cell * 0.5f ? distance : null;
		}

		public void HoverEnter(Hand hand)
		{
			Hovers++;
			hand.Pulse(HoverPulse, HoverPulseSeconds);
		}

		public void HoverExit(Hand hand) => Hovers--;

		public IInteractable BeginGrab(Hand hand)
		{
			IInteractable spawned = entry.Spawn(hand);
			owner._spawned[spawned] = entry;
			hand.Pulse(SpawnPulse, SpawnPulseSeconds);
			return spawned;
		}
	}

	private readonly List<Slot> _slots = [];
	/// <summary>Everything currently out of the box that came from it, and the entry that made it.</summary>
	private readonly Dictionary<IInteractable, Entry> _spawned = [];
	private readonly Node3D _panel = new() { Name = "Panel", Visible = false };
	private bool _open;
	/// <summary>Per hand (left, right): whether it was carrying a prop from the box inside the chest on the previous frame.</summary>
	private readonly bool[] _carryingInside = new bool[2];

	/// <summary>Where icons register so they can be hovered and gripped; set before adding items.</summary>
	public InteractionManager Interaction { get; set; }

	/// <summary>How far in metres beyond the model a hand still touches it, so the edge is easy to find.</summary>
	private const float TouchMargin = 0.01f;

	/// <summary>The chest model's bounds in this node's local space, grown by <see cref="TouchMargin"/>. A hand touches the chest inside this box.</summary>
	private readonly Aabb _touchBox;

	public override (Vector3 Centre, float Radius) LocalSphere => (_touchBox.GetCenter(), _touchBox.Size.Length() * 0.5f);

	/// <summary>True if a world point is inside the touch box, whatever the chest's position, turn and scale.</summary>
	private bool Touches(Vector3 world) => _touchBox.HasPoint(ToLocal(world));

	public override float? HitTest(Hand hand) =>
		hand.HasTracking && Touches(hand.TipWorld) ? hand.TipWorld.DistanceTo(ToGlobal(_touchBox.GetCenter())) : null;

	public ToyBox()
	{
		Name = "ToyBox";
		_panel.Position = new Vector3(0f, GridBase, 0f);
		AddChild(_panel);
		float scale = WidthMetres / ModelWidth;
		Node3D model = GD.Load<PackedScene>("res://assets/chest/chest.glb").Instantiate<Node3D>();
		model.Scale = Vector3.One * scale;
		model.Position = new Vector3(0f, ModelBaseBelowOrigin * scale, 0f);
		model.Rotation = new Vector3(0f, FrontTurn, 0f);
		AddChild(model);
		_touchBox = (ModelProp.MeshBounds(model, Transform3D.Identity) ?? new Aabb(Vector3.Zero, Vector3.One * WidthMetres)).Grow(TouchMargin);
		// Nothing in the scene is lit but the voxels, whose shader does its own lighting, so show the pixel-art texture as is
		foreach (Node child in model.FindChildren("*", nameof(MeshInstance3D), true, false))
		{
			MeshInstance3D mesh = (MeshInstance3D)child;
			if (mesh.GetActiveMaterial(0) is BaseMaterial3D source)
			{
				BaseMaterial3D material = (BaseMaterial3D)source.Duplicate();
				material.ShadingMode = BaseMaterial3D.ShadingModeEnum.Unshaded;
				material.TextureFilter = BaseMaterial3D.TextureFilterEnum.Nearest;
				mesh.MaterialOverride = material;
			}
		}
	}

	/// <summary>Adds an icon to the end of the grid, which re-lays itself out.</summary>
	/// <param name="makeIcon">Makes a fresh model of the icon, measured and scaled to fit whatever its size.</param>
	/// <param name="grab">The grab mode of what <paramref name="spawn"/> returns, so the right kind of grab starts.</param>
	/// <param name="tiltDegrees">Leans the icon over so turning it shows something even for an upright object.</param>
	public void AddItem(string name, Func<Node3D> makeIcon, GrabMode grab, Func<Hand, IInteractable> spawn, Action<IInteractable> despawn, float tiltDegrees = 0f)
	{
		Node3D tilt = new() { Name = "Tilt", Rotation = new Vector3(0f, 0f, Mathf.DegToRad(tiltDegrees)) };
		tilt.AddChild(MiniIcon.Fit(makeIcon(), IconDiagonal));
		Node3D spinner = new() { Name = "Spinner" };
		spinner.AddChild(tilt);
		Node3D node = new() { Name = name };
		node.AddChild(spinner);
		_panel.AddChild(node);
		Slot slot = new(this, new Entry(name, grab, spawn, despawn), node, spinner);
		_slots.Add(slot);
		Interaction?.Register(slot);
		// Rows fill left to right and stack upward; a short last row is centred
		for (int i = 0; i < _slots.Count; i++)
		{
			int row = i / Columns;
			int inRow = Math.Min(Columns, _slots.Count - row * Columns);
			_slots[i].Node.Position = new Vector3((i % Columns - (inRow - 1) / 2f) * Cell, row * Cell, 0f);
		}
	}

	public override void HoverEnter(Hand hand) => hand.Pulse(TouchPulse, TouchPulseSeconds);

	public override void TriggerDown(Hand hand)
	{
		_panel.Visible = _open = !_open;
		hand.Pulse(ClickPulse, ClickPulseSeconds);
	}

	/// <summary>Turns the grid toward the viewer about the vertical and holds it above the chest. The grid is always life size, whatever the chest's scale. Call once per frame.</summary>
	public void Face(Vector3 headPosition)
	{
		Vector3 toHead = headPosition - GlobalPosition;
		toHead.Y = 0f;
		if (!_open || toHead.LengthSquared() < 1e-4f)
			return;
		_panel.GlobalTransform = new Transform3D(
			new Basis(Vector3.Up, Mathf.Atan2(toHead.X, toHead.Z)),
			ToGlobal(new Vector3(0f, GridBase, 0f)));
	}

	/// <summary>Puts away a prop that came from the box if it was let go with the hand on the chest. Wire to <see cref="InteractionManager.Released"/>.</summary>
	public void OnReleased(IInteractable item, Hand hand)
	{
		if (!_spawned.TryGetValue(item, out Entry entry) || !Touches(hand.TipWorld))
			return;
		_spawned.Remove(item);
		entry.Despawn(item);
	}

	/// <summary>Buzzes each hand that is carrying a prop from the box, according to whether it is inside the chest.</summary>
	private void UpdateCarrying()
	{
		if (Interaction is null)
			return;
		for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
		{
			Hand hand = i == 0 ? Interaction.Left : Interaction.Right;
			IInteractable carried = hand.GripPressed && hand.Hovered is { } held && _spawned.ContainsKey(held) ? held : null;
			bool inside = carried is not null && Touches(hand.TipWorld);
			// A prop that would be put away if let go now is hidden, so it is clear it is on its way into the chest
			if (carried is not null)
				carried.Node.Visible = !inside;
			// The same buzz as an empty hand touching the chest
			if (inside && !_carryingInside[i])
				hand.Pulse(TouchPulse, TouchPulseSeconds);
			_carryingInside[i] = inside;
		}
	}

	public override void _Process(double delta)
	{
		UpdateCarrying();
		if (!_open)
			return;
		float blend = 1f - Mathf.Exp(-ScaleRate * (float)delta);
		foreach (Slot slot in _slots)
		{
			bool hovered = slot.Hovers > 0;
			slot.Node.Scale = slot.Node.Scale.Lerp(Vector3.One * (hovered ? HoveredScale : 1f), blend);
			slot.Angle = hovered ? slot.Angle + SpinRadiansPerSecond * (float)delta : Mathf.LerpAngle(slot.Angle, 0f, blend);
			slot.Spinner.Rotation = new Vector3(0f, slot.Angle, 0f);
		}
	}
}
```
